      kind, path, terms, sender, weight: 1, note: item.subject || '', createdAt: Date.now(),
    });
  }
  // A thumbs-down cancels an earlier thumbs-up for the same email terms, and vice versa.
  const opposite = kind === 'prefer' ? 'avoid' : 'prefer';
  const kept = rules.filter((r) => !(r.path === path && r.kind === opposite
    && r.terms.filter((t) => terms.includes(t)).length >= Math.min(3, terms.length)));
  await saveRules(kept);
  return kept;
}

// Free-text hint: "Concrete strength questions → Specs/03 30 00.pdf"
export async function addHint(text, path, kind = 'prefer') {
  const rules = await getRules();
  const terms = [...new Set(tokenize(text))].slice(0, 12);
  if (!terms.length || !path) throw new Error('A hint needs some keywords and a document');
  rules.push({
    id: `r${Date.now().toString(36)}${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)}`,
    kind, path, terms, sender: '', weight: 2, note: text, createdAt: Date.now(),
  });
  await saveRules(rules);
  return rules;
}

export async function deleteRule(id) {
  const rules = (await getRules()).filter((r) => r.id !== id);
  await saveRules(rules);
  return rules;
}

// Returns {prefer: [path...], avoid: Set(path)} for an email.
export async function matchRules(queryTerms, from) {
  const rules = await getRules();
  if (!rules.length) return { prefer: [], avoid: new Set() };
  const q = new Set(queryTerms instanceof Map ? queryTerms.keys() : queryTerms);
  const dom = domainOf(from);
  const scored = [];
  for (const r of rules) {
    const overlap = r.terms.filter((t) => q.has(t)).length;
    const senderHit = r.sender && dom && r.sender === dom;
    const need = Math.min(2, r.terms.length);
    const matched = overlap >= need || (senderHit && overlap >= 1);
    if (!matched) continue;
    // Skip rules whose document no longer exists in the index.
    if (!(await get('files', r.path))) continue;
    scored.push([r, (overlap / Math.max(3, Math.min(r.terms.length, 8))) * (r.weight || 1) + (senderHit ? 0.5 : 0)]);
  }
  scored.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]);
  const prefer = [];
  const avoid = new Set();
  for (const [r] of scored) {
    if (r.kind === 'avoid') avoid.add(r.path);
    else if (!prefer.includes(r.path)) prefer.push(r.path);
  }
  return { prefer: prefer.filter((p) => !avoid.has(p)).slice(0, 3), avoid };
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/text.js (219 lines) =====
// Tokenizing, chunking and email cleanup. Pure functions, no browser APIs,
// so the same code runs in the engine, the side panel and the tests.

const STOP = new Set(`a about above after again against all am an and any are as at be because been
before being below between both but by can could did do does doing down during each few for from
further had has have having he her here hers him his how i if in into is it its itself just me more
most my no nor not now of off on once only or other our ours out over own same she should so some
such than that the their theirs them then there these they this those through to too under until up
very was we were what when where which while who whom why will with would you your yours yourself
re fw fwd hi hello dear thanks thank regards best please kind cheers also get got let us may might
must shall per via etc ok yes`.split(/\s+/));

const WORD = /[\p{L}\p{N}]+(?:[-./_][\p{L}\p{N}]+)*/gu;

function stem(w) {
  if (w.length > 4 && /\p{L}$/u.test(w)) {
    if (w.endsWith('ies') && w.length > 5) return w.slice(0, -3) + 'y';
    if (w.endsWith('sses')) return w.slice(0, -2);
    if (w.endsWith('s') && !w.endsWith('ss') && !w.endsWith('us') && !w.endsWith('is')) return w.slice(0, -1);
  }
  return w;
}

// Returns index terms. Compound codes like "24-0173" or "A-201" are kept whole
// and also split into parts, so both "24-0173" and "0173" match.
export function tokenize(text) {
  const out = [];
  if (!text) return out;
  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  for (const m of lower.matchAll(WORD)) {
    const tok = m[0];
    if (/[-./_]/.test(tok)) {
      if (tok.length <= 40 && /\p{N}/u.test(tok)) out.push(tok);
      for (const part of tok.split(/[-./_]+/)) addWord(part, out);
    } else {
      addWord(tok, out);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function addWord(w, out) {
  if (!w || w.length > 40) return;
  if (w.length < 2 && !/\p{N}/u.test(w)) return;
  if (STOP.has(w)) return;
  out.push(stem(w));
}

export function termFreqs(text) {
  const tf = new Map();
  for (const t of tokenize(text)) tf.set(t, (tf.get(t) || 0) + 1);
  return tf;
}

// Splits text into ~target-sized chunks on paragraph, then sentence boundaries.
// pages: optional array of [pageNumber, text]; chunks remember their start page.
export function chunkText(textOrPages, target = 1100) {
  const pages = Array.isArray(textOrPages) ? textOrPages : [[0, textOrPages || '']];
  const chunks = [];
  let buf = '';
  let bufPage = null;
  const flush = () => {
    const t = buf.replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
    if (t.length >= 3) chunks.push({ text: t, page: bufPage || 0 });
    buf = '';
    bufPage = null;
  };
  for (const [page, text] of pages) {
    // A chunk never spans pages (so citations name the right page), except
    // that near-empty pages are folded into the next one.
    if (buf && bufPage !== page && buf.length >= 80) flush();
    const paras = String(text || '').split(/\n\s*\n|\f/);
    for (let p of paras) {
      p = p.trim();
      if (!p) continue;
      while (p.length > target * 1.5) {
        // Long paragraph: cut at the last sentence end before the target.
        let cut = p.lastIndexOf('. ', target);
        if (cut < target * 0.5) cut = p.lastIndexOf(' ', target);
        if (cut < target * 0.5) cut = target;
        if (buf) flush();
        bufPage = page;
        buf = p.slice(0, cut + 1);
        flush();
        p = p.slice(cut + 1).trim();
      }
      if (buf && buf.length + p.length + 2 > target) flush();
      if (!buf) bufPage = page;
      buf += (buf ? '\n\n' : '') + p;
    }
  }
  if (buf) flush();
  return chunks;
}

// ---- email cleanup (saves tokens: drop quoted history, signatures, disclaimers) ----

const QUOTE_MARKERS = [
  /^-{2,}\s*Original Message\s*-{2,}/im,
  /^_{10,}\s*$/m,
  /^From:\s.+\n(?:.*\n){0,3}?(?:Sent|Date):\s.+/im,
  /^On .{6,120}\bwrote:\s*$/im,
  /^De\s?:\s.+\n(?:.*\n){0,3}?(?:Envoy[ée]|Date)\s?:\s.+/im,
  /^Le .{6,120}a écrit\s?:\s*$/im,
];

const DISCLAIMER = /(this (e-?mail|message)( and any attachments)? (is|are|may be) (confidential|intended)|ce (courriel|message) (est|peut être) confidentiel|please consider the environment before printing|caution: this email originated from outside)/i;

export function cleanEmailBody(body, maxChars = 6000) {
  if (!body) return '';
  let text = String(body).replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').replace(/ /g, ' ');
  let cut = text.length;
  for (const re of QUOTE_MARKERS) {
    const m = re.exec(text);
    if (m && m.index > 20 && m.index < cut) cut = m.index;
  }
  text = text.slice(0, cut);
  text = text.split('\n').filter((l) => !/^\s*>/.test(l)).join('\n');
  const d = DISCLAIMER.exec(text);
  if (d && d.index > 40) text = text.slice(0, d.index);
  const sig = /^\s*(--|__)\s*$/m.exec(text);
  if (sig && sig.index > 40) text = text.slice(0, sig.index);
  text = text.replace(/[ \t]+/g, ' ').replace(/\n[ \t]+/g, '\n').replace(/\n{3,}/g, '\n\n').trim();
  if (text.length > maxChars) text = text.slice(0, maxChars) + '\n[…email truncated]';
  return text;
}

export function splitList(s) {
  return String(s || '').split(/[,\n]/).map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

// Glob-lite: "*" wildcard, matched against each path segment and the whole path.
export function makeMatcher(patterns) {
  const res = splitList(patterns).map((p) => {
    const re = p.replace(/[.+^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&').replace(/\*/g, '.*').replace(/\?/g, '.');
    return new RegExp(`^${re}$`, 'i');
  });
  return (path) => {
    if (!res.length) return false;
    const segs = path.split('/');
    return res.some((re) => re.test(path) || segs.some((s) => re.test(s)));
  };
}

/** "p. 3" for documents, "email 3" for a mailbox (.pst) whose "pages" are emails. */
export function pageLabel(path, page) {
  if (!page) return '';
  return /\.pst$/i.test(path || '') ? `email ${page}` : `p. ${page}`;
}

/**
 * An email copied from the Outlook desktop app (or anywhere): pulls From / Sent / To / Cc / Subject from a header
 * block at the top if there is one (English or French Outlook), the rest is the body.
 */
export function parseCopiedEmail(text) {
  const t = String(text || '').replace(/\r\n?/g, '\n').replace(/\u00a0/g, ' ').trim();
  const out = { from: '', to: '', cc: '', subject: '', receivedAt: '', body: t };
  const keys = {
    from: /^(from|de)\s*:\s*(.*)$/i, sent: /^(sent|date|envoy[ée]|re[çc]u)\s*:\s*(.*)$/i, to: /^(to|[àa])\s*:\s*(.*)$/i,
    cc: /^cc\s*:\s*(.*)$/i, subject: /^(subject|objet)\s*:\s*(.*)$/i,
  };
  const lines = t.split('\n');
  let i = 0;
  let found = 0;
  for (; i < Math.min(lines.length, 14); i++) {
    const l = lines[i].trim();
    if (!l) {
      if (found) break;
      continue;
    }
    let m;
    if ((m = keys.from.exec(l))) out.from = m[2].trim();
    else if ((m = keys.sent.exec(l))) out.receivedAt = m[2].trim();
    else if ((m = keys.to.exec(l))) out.to = m[2].trim();
    else if ((m = keys.cc.exec(l))) out.cc = m[1].trim();
    else if ((m = keys.subject.exec(l))) out.subject = m[2].trim();
    else if (found) break;
    else continue;
    found++;
  }
  if (found >= 2) out.body = lines.slice(i).join('\n').trim();
  return out;
}

export function extOf(name) {
  const i = name.lastIndexOf('.');
  return i > 0 ? name.slice(i + 1).toLowerCase() : '';
}

export function projectOf(path, depth = 1) {
  const segs = path.split('/');
  if (segs.length <= depth) return '';
  return segs.slice(0, depth).join('/');
}

export function hashString(s) {
  // FNV-1a, 52-bit safe; stable ids for emails captured without a server id.
  let h1 = 0x811c9dc5, h2 = 0x1000193;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    const c = s.charCodeAt(i);
    h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ c, 16777619) >>> 0;
    h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ c, 2246822519) >>> 0;
  }
  return h1.toString(36) + h2.toString(36);
}

export function decodeXmlEntities(s) {
  return s.replace(/&(#x[0-9a-f]+|#\d+|amp|lt|gt|quot|apos|nbsp);/gi, (m, e) => {
    const l = e.toLowerCase();
    if (l === 'amp') return '&';
    if (l === 'lt') return '<';
    if (l === 'gt') return '>';
    if (l === 'quot') return '"';
    if (l === 'apos') return "'";
    if (l === 'nbsp') return ' ';
    const code = l.startsWith('#x') ? parseInt(l.slice(2), 16) : parseInt(l.slice(1), 10);
    try { return String.fromCodePoint(code); } catch { return ' '; }
  });
}

===== FILE: extension/lib/transfer.js (728 lines) =====
// Transfer queue: parallel uploads, server-side moves and deletions (for mirrors), retries with back-off,
// pause/resume/cancel, skip-existing (resume) and verification.

import { WebDAVError } from './webdav.js';
import { joinPath, parentPath, baseName, normalizePath, sleep, after, waitForOnline } from './util.js';

let nextId = 1;

/** True when the NAS copy was written after the local file was last modified (HTTP dates have 1 s resolution). */
export function remoteIsUpToDate(remoteModified, localMtime) {
  if (!remoteModified || isNaN(remoteModified)) return true;
  return remoteModified.getTime() >= Math.floor(localMtime / 1000) * 1000;
}

export class TransferManager extends EventTarget {
  /**
   * @param {import('./webdav.js').WebDAVClient} client
   * @param {{concurrency?: number, skipExisting?: boolean, verify?: boolean, maxAttempts?: number, retryBaseMs?: number, autoResumeMs?: number}} options
   */
  constructor(client, options = {}) {
    super();
    this.client = client;
    this.options = { concurrency: 3, skipExisting: true, verify: true, maxAttempts: 8, retryBaseMs: 2000, autoResumeMs: 60000, ...options };
    this.state = 'idle'; // idle | running | paused | verifying | done | cancelled
    this.runs = 0; // incremented every time a new run starts (used by the UI and tests)
    this.connectionIssue = null; // {message, resumeAt} while auto-paused because the NAS is unreachable
    this.autoResumeTimer = null;
    this.reset();
  }

  reset() {
    this.tasks = [];
    this.queue = [];
    this.qi = 0;
    this.front = [];
    this.deferred = []; // deletions: only run once every upload and move has finished
    this.active = new Set();
    this.dirMemo = new Map(); // folder -> Promise<{created}>
    this.listMemo = new Map(); // folder -> Promise<Map(name -> entry)>
    this.knownDirs = new Set(); // folders known to exist
    this.knownListings = new Map(); // folder -> Map(name -> entry), known without asking the NAS
    this.createdDirs = new Set();
    this.batches = []; // mirror batches, kept until the next fresh session
    this.session = new AbortController();
    this.sentBytes = 0;
    this.samples = [];
    this.startedAt = 0;
    this.finishedAt = 0;
    this.verifying = false;
    this.verifyProgress = null;
  }

  setClient(client) {
    this.client = client;
  }

  emit() {
    this.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
  }

  setState(s) {
    if (this.state !== s) {
      if (s === 'running' && !this.busy) this.runs++;
      this.state = s;
      this.emit();
    }
  }

  get busy() {
    return this.state === 'running' || this.state === 'paused' || this.state === 'verifying';
  }

  startSessionIfIdle() {
    if (this.busy) return;
    // Start a fresh session, but keep earlier failures visible until the user clears them.
    const keep = this.tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'failed');
    this.reset();
    for (const t of keep) this.tasks.push(t);
    this.startedAt = Date.now();
  }

  newTask(fields) {
    const t = { id: nextId++, status: 'pending', loaded: 0, size: 0, error: null, note: null, ...fields };
    this.tasks.push(t);
    return t;
  }

  start() {
    if (this.state !== 'paused') this.setState('running');
    this.emit();
    this.pump();
  }

  /**
   * Queue a scanned batch for upload into destRoot.
   * @param {{files: {rel: string, file: File}[], dirs: string[]}} scan
   * @param {string} destRoot existing folder on the NAS
   */
  addBatch(scan, destRoot) {
    this.startSessionIfIdle();
    destRoot = normalizePath(destRoot);
    this.knownDirs.add(destRoot);

    const fileDirs = new Set();
    for (const { rel, file } of scan.files) {
      const destPath = joinPath(destRoot, rel);
      const task = this.newTask({ kind: 'file', rel, name: baseName(destPath), destPath, destDir: parentPath(destPath), file, size: file.size, expectSize: file.size, verified: false });
      this.queue.push(task);
      for (let d = task.destDir; d !== '/' && !fileDirs.has(d); d = parentPath(d)) fileDirs.add(d);
    }
    // Folders with no files inside them still need to exist on the NAS.
    for (const rel of scan.dirs) {
      const destPath = joinPath(destRoot, rel);
      if (fileDirs.has(destPath)) continue;
      this.queue.push(this.newTask({ kind: 'dir', rel: rel + '/', name: baseName(destPath), destPath, destDir: parentPath(destPath) }));
    }
    this.start();
  }

  /**
   * Queue a mirror plan (from mirror.js planMirror) that makes the NAS folder `root` match the local folder.
   * Uploads and moves run first; deletions run last, once everything else has finished.
   * @returns {{kind: 'mirror', root: string, plan: object, remote: object, tasks: object[]}}
   */
  addMirror(plan, root, remote, extra = {}) {
    this.startSessionIfIdle();
    root = normalizePath(root);
    const batch = { kind: 'mirror', root, plan, remote, tasks: [], ...extra };
    const mk = (fields) => {
      const t = this.newTask({ ...fields, batch });
      batch.tasks.push(t);
      return t;
    };

    // Seed what the listing already told us, so the queue doesn't have to ask the NAS again.
    if (remote.exists) {
      this.knownDirs.add(root);
      const listings = new Map([[root, new Map()]]);
      for (const d of remote.dirs.values()) {
        const abs = joinPath(root, d.rel);
        this.knownDirs.add(abs);
        if (d.listed) listings.set(abs, new Map());
      }
      for (const d of remote.dirs.values()) {
        const abs = joinPath(root, d.rel);
        listings.get(parentPath(abs))?.set(baseName(abs), { name: baseName(abs), isDir: true });
      }
      for (const f of remote.files.values()) {
        const abs = joinPath(root, f.rel);
        listings.get(parentPath(abs))?.set(baseName(abs), { name: baseName(abs), isDir: false, size: f.size, modified: f.modified });
      }
      for (const [k, v] of listings) this.knownListings.set(k, v);
    } else {
      this.queue.push(mk({ kind: 'dir', rel: baseName(root) + '/', name: baseName(root), destPath: root, destDir: parentPath(root) }));
    }

    const fileDirs = new Set();
    const noteDirs = (destDir) => {
      for (let d = destDir; d !== '/' && !fileDirs.has(d); d = parentPath(d)) fileDirs.add(d);
    };
    for (const m of plan.moves) {
      const to = joinPath(root, m.to);
      const t = mk({
        kind: 'move',
        rel: m.to,
        name: baseName(to),
        from: joinPath(root, m.from),
        destPath: to,
        destDir: parentPath(to),
        file: m.file,
        expectSize: m.file.size,
        verified: false,
        mirrorRel: m.to,
        mirrorFrom: m.from,
        localSize: m.file.size,
        localMtime: m.file.lastModified,
      });
      this.queue.push(t);
      noteDirs(t.destDir);
    }
    for (const u of plan.uploads) {
      const destPath = joinPath(root, u.rel);
      const t = mk({
        kind: 'file',
        force: true,
        reason: u.reason,
        rel: u.rel,
        name: baseName(destPath),
        destPath,
        destDir: parentPath(destPath),
        file: u.file,
        size: u.file.size,
        expectSize: u.file.size,
        verified: false,
        mirrorRel: u.rel,
        localSize: u.file.size,
        localMtime: u.file.lastModified,
      });
      this.queue.push(t);
      noteDirs(t.destDir);
    }
    for (const rel of plan.createDirs) {
      const destPath = joinPath(root, rel);
      if (fileDirs.has(destPath)) continue;
      this.queue.push(mk({ kind: 'dir', rel: rel + '/', name: baseName(destPath), destPath, destDir: parentPath(destPath) }));
    }
    for (const d of plan.deleteDirs) {
      const p = joinPath(root, d.rel);
      this.deferred.push(mk({ kind: 'delete', dir: true, rel: d.rel + '/', name: baseName(p), destPath: p, destDir: parentPath(p), mirrorRel: d.rel, count: d.files }));
    }
    for (const d of plan.deletes) {
      const p = joinPath(root, d.rel);
      this.deferred.push(mk({ kind: 'delete', dir: false, rel: d.rel, name: baseName(p), destPath: p, destDir: parentPath(p), mirrorRel: d.rel, count: 1 }));
    }
    this.batches.push(batch);
    this.start();
    return batch;
  }

  // ---- queue mechanics ---------------------------------------------------

  nextPending() {
    while (this.front.length) {
      const t = this.front.shift();
      if (t.status === 'pending') return t;
    }
    while (this.qi < this.queue.length) {
      const t = this.queue[this.qi++];
      if (t.status === 'pending') return t;
    }
    // Compact the queue so it doesn't grow forever across retries.
    if (this.qi > 0) {
      this.queue = [];
      this.qi = 0;
    }
    return null;
  }

  hasPending() {
    return this.front.length > 0 || this.qi < this.queue.length;
  }

  pump() {
    if (this.state !== 'running') return;
    for (;;) {
      while (this.active.size < this.options.concurrency) {
        const task = this.nextPending();
        if (!task) break;
        this.runTask(task);
      }
      if (this.active.size === 0 && !this.hasPending()) {
        if (this.deferred.length) {
          for (const t of this.deferred) this.queue.push(t);
          this.deferred = [];
          continue;
        }
        this.finishRun();
      }
      return;
    }
  }

  async retry(fn, { signal, onRetry, retryIf = (err) => err?.retryable } = {}) {
    let attempt = 0;
    for (;;) {
      attempt++;
      try {
        return await fn();
      } catch (err) {
        if (err?.aborted || signal?.aborted) throw err;
        if (!retryIf(err) || attempt >= this.options.maxAttempts) throw err;
        if (!navigator.onLine) {
          onRetry?.(err, attempt, 0, true);
          await waitForOnline(signal);
        }
        const base = Math.min(60000, this.options.retryBaseMs * 2 ** (attempt - 1));
        const delay = Math.round(base * (0.75 + Math.random() * 0.5));
        onRetry?.(err, attempt, delay, false);
        await sleep(delay, signal);
      }
    }
  }

  /** Make sure a folder exists on the NAS; resolves to {created}. */
  prepareDir(dir) {
    dir = normalizePath(dir);
    let p = this.dirMemo.get(dir);
    if (!p) {
      p = this._prepareDir(dir);
      this.dirMemo.set(dir, p);
      p.catch(() => {
        if (this.dirMemo.get(dir) === p) this.dirMemo.delete(dir);
      });
    }
    return p;
  }

  async _prepareDir(dir) {
    const signal = this.session.signal;
    if (dir === '/' || this.knownDirs.has(dir)) return { created: false };
    const parentDir = parentPath(dir);
    const parent = await this.prepareDir(parentDir);
    if (!parent.created) {
      // If we know (or are going to fetch anyway) what the parent holds, skip asking to create existing folders.
      const listing = this.knownListings.get(parentDir) || (this.options.skipExisting ? await this.listDir(parentDir) : null);
      const e = listing?.get(baseName(dir));
      if (e && !e.isDir) throw new WebDAVError(`A file named "${baseName(dir)}" already exists on the NAS where a folder is needed`);
      if (e) return { created: false };
    }
    const r = await this.retry(() => this.client.mkdir(dir, { signal }), { signal });
    if (r === 'created') {
      this.createdDirs.add(dir);
      return { created: true };
    }
    return { created: false };
  }

  /** Children of a NAS folder (memoised; folders created in this session are known to be empty). */
  listDir(dir) {
    dir = normalizePath(dir);
    if (this.knownListings.has(dir)) return Promise.resolve(this.knownListings.get(dir));
    if (this.createdDirs.has(dir)) return Promise.resolve(new Map());
    let p = this.listMemo.get(dir);
    if (!p) {
      const signal = this.session.signal;
      p = this.retry(() => this.client.list(dir, { signal }), { signal }).then((entries) => new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.name, e])));
      this.listMemo.set(dir, p);
      p.catch(() => {
        if (this.listMemo.get(dir) === p) this.listMemo.delete(dir);
      });
    }
    return p;
  }

  uploadTask(task, signal) {
    return this.retry(
      () => {
        task.loaded = 0;
        return this.client.upload(task.destPath, task.file, {
          signal,
          onProgress: (loaded) => {
            const delta = loaded - task.loaded;
            if (delta > 0) this.sentBytes += delta;
            task.loaded = loaded;
          },
        });
      },
      {
        signal,
        onRetry: (err, attempt, delay, offline) => {
          task.note = offline ? 'Waiting for internet connection…' : `Retry ${attempt}/${this.options.maxAttempts - 1} in ${Math.round(delay / 1000)}s — ${err.message}`;
          this.emit();
        },
      },
    );
  }

  async runTask(task) {
    task.status = 'active';
    task.loaded = 0;
    task.note = null;
    task.error = null;
    task.pauseRequested = false;
    task.controller = new AbortController();
    const session = this.session;
    const onSessionAbort = () => task.controller?.abort();
    session.signal.addEventListener('abort', onSessionAbort, { once: true });
    this.active.add(task);
    const signal = task.controller.signal;
    const aborted = () => new WebDAVError('Cancelled', { aborted: true });
    try {
      if (task.kind === 'dir') {
        await this.prepareDir(task.destPath);
        task.status = 'done';
      } else if (task.kind === 'delete') {
        await this.retry(() => this.client.remove(task.destPath, { dir: task.dir, ignoreMissing: true, signal }), { signal });
        task.status = 'done';
      } else if (task.kind === 'move') {
        await this.prepareDir(task.destDir);
        if (signal.aborted) throw aborted();
        try {
          // A refused move (e.g. 502 "Destination refers to a different scheme" behind a proxy) won't succeed on
          // retry; only connection trouble and "busy" answers are worth retrying before falling back.
          await this.retry(() => this.client.move(task.from, task.destPath, { signal }), {
            signal,
            retryIf: (e) => e?.status === 0 || e?.status === 503 || e?.status === 429,
          });
        } catch (err) {
          if (err?.aborted || signal.aborted || err?.status === 0) throw err;
          // The NAS refused the server-side move (some do behind proxies): send the file instead, then remove
          // the old copy along with the other deletions.
          task.fellBack = true;
          task.size = task.file.size;
          task.note = 'The NAS could not move it, uploading instead…';
          this.emit();
          await this.uploadTask(task, signal);
          const old = this.newTask({
            kind: 'delete',
            dir: false,
            rel: task.mirrorFrom || task.from,
            name: baseName(task.from),
            destPath: task.from,
            destDir: parentPath(task.from),
            batch: task.batch,
            mirrorRel: task.mirrorFrom,
            count: 1,
          });
          task.batch?.tasks.push(old);
          this.deferred.push(old);
        }
        task.status = 'done';
      } else {
        const dirInfo = await this.prepareDir(task.destDir);
        if (signal.aborted) throw aborted();
        if (!task.force && this.options.skipExisting) {
          const listing = dirInfo.created ? null : await this.listDir(task.destDir);
          const existing = listing?.get(task.name);
          if (existing && existing.isDir) throw new WebDAVError(`A folder named "${task.name}" already exists on the NAS where this file should go`);
          if (existing && existing.size === task.size && remoteIsUpToDate(existing.modified, task.file.lastModified)) {
            task.status = 'skipped';
            return;
          }
        }
        await this.uploadTask(task, signal);
        task.status = 'done';
        task.verified = false;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      if (task.pauseRequested) {
        task.status = 'pending';
        this.front.push(task);
      } else if (session.signal.aborted) {
        task.status = 'cancelled';
      } else if (err?.retryable && err.status === 0 && (task.netRounds = (task.netRounds || 0) + 1) < 3) {
        // The NAS stayed unreachable through every retry. Don't burn through the whole queue marking files as
        // failed: put this one at the back, pause, and try again automatically in a minute.
        task.status = 'pending';
        if (task.kind === 'delete') this.deferred.push(task);
        else this.queue.push(task);
        this.connectionLost(err);
      } else {
        task.status = 'failed';
        task.error = err?.message || String(err);
      }
    } finally {
      session.signal.removeEventListener('abort', onSessionAbort);
      task.loaded = 0;
      task.controller = null;
      task.note = null;
      // Release the File reference once it is safely on the NAS.
      if (task.status === 'done' && !this.options.verify) task.file = null;
      this.active.delete(task);
      this.emit();
      this.pump();
    }
  }

  async finishRun() {
    if (this.state !== 'running' || this.verifying) return;
    if (this.options.verify) {
      const toVerify = this.tasks.filter((t) => (t.kind === 'file' || t.kind === 'move') && t.status === 'done' && !t.verified);
      if (toVerify.length) {
        this.verifying = true;
        this.setState('verifying');
        try {
          await this.verify(toVerify);
        } finally {
          this.verifying = false;
        }
        if (this.state === 'verifying') this.setState('running');
        else if (this.state !== 'running') return; // cancelled meanwhile
        // More files may have been dropped while verifying; pump() finishes the run once everything is idle.
        this.pump();
        return;
      }
    }
    this.finishedAt = Date.now();
    this.setState('done');
    this.settled();
  }

  /** Tell listeners (mirror bookkeeping) that a run has ended, successfully or not. */
  settled() {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('settled', { detail: { batches: this.batches.slice() } }));
  }

  /** Re-list every folder we wrote into and confirm each file is there with the right size. */
  async verify(tasks) {
    const byDir = new Map();
    for (const t of tasks) {
      if (!byDir.has(t.destDir)) byDir.set(t.destDir, []);
      byDir.get(t.destDir).push(t);
    }
    const dirs = [...byDir.keys()];
    this.verifyProgress = { done: 0, total: dirs.length };
    const signal = this.session.signal;
    let i = 0;
    const worker = async () => {
      while (i < dirs.length && !signal.aborted) {
        const dir = dirs[i++];
        const group = byDir.get(dir);
        try {
          const entries = await this.retry(() => this.client.list(dir, { signal }), { signal });
          const map = new Map(entries.map((e) => [e.name, e]));
          for (const t of group) {
            const e = map.get(t.name);
            const expected = t.expectSize ?? t.size;
            if (!e || e.isDir) {
              t.status = 'failed';
              t.error = 'Verification failed: file is missing on the NAS afterwards';
            } else if (e.size !== null && e.size !== expected) {
              t.status = 'failed';
              t.error = `Verification failed: NAS has ${e.size} bytes, expected ${expected}`;
            } else {
              t.verified = true;
              t.file = null;
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          if (signal.aborted) return;
          for (const t of group) {
            t.status = 'failed';
            t.error = 'Could not verify: ' + (err?.message || err);
          }
        }
        this.verifyProgress.done++;
        this.emit();
      }
    };
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(4, dirs.length) }, worker));
  }

  connectionLost(err) {
    if (this.state !== 'running') return;
    const delay = this.options.autoResumeMs;
    this.connectionIssue = { message: err?.message || String(err), resumeAt: Date.now() + delay };
    this.setState('paused');
    this.autoResumeTimer?.();
    this.autoResumeTimer = after(delay, () => {
      if (this.state === 'paused' && this.connectionIssue) this.resume();
    });
  }

  clearConnectionIssue() {
    this.autoResumeTimer?.();
    this.autoResumeTimer = null;
    if (this.connectionIssue) {
      this.connectionIssue = null;
      this.emit();
    }
  }

  // ---- user controls -----------------------------------------------------

  pause() {
    if (this.state !== 'running') return;
    this.clearConnectionIssue();
    this.setState('paused');
    for (const t of this.active) {
      if (t.kind === 'file' || (t.kind === 'move' && t.fellBack)) {
        t.pauseRequested = true;
        t.controller?.abort();
      }
    }
  }

  resume() {
    if (this.state !== 'paused') return;
    this.clearConnectionIssue();
    this.setState('running');
    this.pump();
  }

  cancel() {
    if (!this.busy) return;
    this.clearConnectionIssue();
    this.session.abort();
    for (const t of this.tasks) if (t.status === 'pending') t.status = 'cancelled';
    this.front = [];
    this.queue = [];
    this.qi = 0;
    this.deferred = [];
    this.finishedAt = Date.now();
    this.setState('cancelled');
    this.session = new AbortController();
    this.dirMemo.clear();
    this.listMemo.clear();
    this.settled();
  }

  retryFailed() {
    const failed = this.tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'failed' || t.status === 'cancelled');
    if (!failed.length) return;
    if (!this.busy) {
      this.session = new AbortController();
      this.dirMemo.clear();
      this.listMemo.clear();
      this.sentBytes = 0;
      this.samples = [];
      this.startedAt = Date.now();
    }
    for (const t of failed) {
      if ((t.kind === 'file' || t.kind === 'move') && !t.file) continue;
      t.status = 'pending';
      t.error = null;
      t.verified = false;
      if (t.kind === 'delete') this.deferred.push(t);
      else this.queue.push(t);
    }
    this.start();
  }

  clearFinished() {
    if (this.busy) return;
    this.reset();
    this.setState('idle');
  }

  // ---- reporting -----------------------------------------------------------

  stats() {
    const s = {
      state: this.state,
      files: 0,
      dirs: 0,
      totalBytes: 0,
      doneFiles: 0,
      doneBytes: 0,
      uploadedFiles: 0,
      uploadedBytes: 0,
      skippedFiles: 0,
      skippedBytes: 0,
      failedFiles: 0,
      cancelledFiles: 0,
      verifiedFiles: 0,
      inflightBytes: 0,
      pendingFiles: 0,
      dirsDone: 0,
      dirsFailed: 0,
      moves: 0,
      movesDone: 0,
      movesFellBack: 0,
      deletes: 0,
      deletesDone: 0,
      deletedFiles: 0,
      opsFailed: 0,
      opsPending: 0,
    };
    for (const t of this.tasks) {
      if (t.kind === 'dir') {
        s.dirs++;
        if (t.status === 'done') s.dirsDone++;
        if (t.status === 'failed') s.dirsFailed++;
        continue;
      }
      if (t.kind === 'delete' || t.kind === 'move') {
        const isMove = t.kind === 'move';
        if (isMove) s.moves++;
        else s.deletes++;
        if (t.status === 'done') {
          if (isMove) {
            s.movesDone++;
            if (t.fellBack) s.movesFellBack++;
            if (t.verified) s.verifiedFiles++;
          } else {
            s.deletesDone++;
            s.deletedFiles += t.count || 1;
          }
        } else if (t.status === 'failed') s.opsFailed++;
        else if (t.status === 'cancelled') s.cancelledFiles++;
        else s.opsPending++;
        if (isMove && t.fellBack) {
          s.totalBytes += t.size;
          if (t.status === 'done') s.doneBytes += t.size;
          if (t.status === 'active') s.inflightBytes += t.loaded;
        }
        continue;
      }
      s.files++;
      s.totalBytes += t.size;
      switch (t.status) {
        case 'done':
          s.doneFiles++;
          s.uploadedFiles++;
          s.doneBytes += t.size;
          s.uploadedBytes += t.size;
          if (t.verified) s.verifiedFiles++;
          break;
        case 'skipped':
          s.doneFiles++;
          s.skippedFiles++;
          s.doneBytes += t.size;
          s.skippedBytes += t.size;
          break;
        case 'failed':
          s.failedFiles++;
          break;
        case 'cancelled':
          s.cancelledFiles++;
          break;
        case 'active':
          s.inflightBytes += t.loaded;
          break;
        default:
          s.pendingFiles++;
      }
    }
    // Speed over the last ~10 seconds of real network transfer.
    const now = Date.now();
    this.samples.push([now, this.sentBytes]);
    while (this.samples.length > 2 && now - this.samples[0][0] > 10000) this.samples.shift();
    const [t0, b0] = this.samples[0];
    s.speed = now - t0 > 500 ? ((this.sentBytes - b0) * 1000) / (now - t0) : 0;
    const remaining = Math.max(0, s.totalBytes - s.doneBytes - s.inflightBytes);
    s.eta = s.speed > 0 ? remaining / s.speed : Infinity;
    s.elapsed = ((this.finishedAt && !this.busy ? this.finishedAt : now) - (this.startedAt || now)) / 1000;
    s.verifyProgress = this.verifyProgress;
    return s;
  }

  activeTasks() {
    return [...this.active];
  }

  failedTasks() {
    return this.tasks.filter((t) => t.status === 'failed');
  }
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/util.js (161 lines) =====
// Small helpers shared by the app: path handling, formatting, auth.

/** Normalise a NAS path to "/a/b" form (always leading slash, no trailing slash, no empty or "." segments). */
export function normalizePath(path) {
  const parts = [];
  for (const seg of String(path || '').split('/')) {
    if (!seg || seg === '.') continue;
    if (seg === '..') parts.pop();
    else parts.push(seg);
  }
  return '/' + parts.join('/');
}

export function joinPath(...parts) {
  return normalizePath(parts.filter((p) => p !== undefined && p !== null && p !== '').join('/'));
}

export function parentPath(path) {
  const p = normalizePath(path);
  if (p === '/') return '/';
  return p.slice(0, p.lastIndexOf('/')) || '/';
}

export function baseName(path) {
  const p = normalizePath(path);
  return p.slice(p.lastIndexOf('/') + 1);
}

/** Percent-encode every segment of a path so names with #, ?, %, spaces or unicode survive the trip. */
export function encodePath(path) {
  return normalizePath(path).split('/').map(encodeURIComponent).join('/');
}

export function safeDecode(s) {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(s);
  } catch {
    return s;
  }
}

export function basicAuthHeader(username, password) {
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(`${username}:${password}`);
  let bin = '';
  for (const b of bytes) bin += String.fromCharCode(b);
  return 'Basic ' + btoa(bin);
}

export function formatBytes(n) {
  if (n === null || n === undefined || isNaN(n)) return '';
  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
  let i = 0;
  let v = n;
  while (v >= 1024 && i < units.length - 1) {
    v /= 1024;
    i++;
  }
  return `${i === 0 ? v : v.toFixed(v >= 100 ? 0 : v >= 10 ? 1 : 2)} ${units[i]}`;
}

export function formatDuration(seconds) {
  if (!isFinite(seconds) || seconds < 0) return '—';
  seconds = Math.round(seconds);
  const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((seconds % 3600) / 60);
  const s = seconds % 60;
  if (h) return `${h}h ${m}m`;
  if (m) return `${m}m ${s}s`;
  return `${s}s`;
}

export function formatDate(d) {
  if (!d || isNaN(d)) return '';
  return d.toLocaleString(undefined, { year: 'numeric', month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
}

// Chrome slows timers in background tabs down to about one per minute. A dedicated worker's timers aren't
// throttled that way, so waits (retry back-off, auto-resume) run on one when possible.
let timerWorker;
let timerSeq = 0;
const timerCallbacks = new Map();
function getTimerWorker() {
  if (timerWorker === undefined) {
    try {
      timerWorker = new Worker(new URL('./timer-worker.js', import.meta.url));
      timerWorker.onmessage = (e) => {
        const fn = timerCallbacks.get(e.data);
        timerCallbacks.delete(e.data);
        fn?.();
      };
    } catch {
      timerWorker = null;
    }
  }
  return timerWorker;
}

/** Run fn after ms milliseconds; returns a function that cancels it. */
export function after(ms, fn) {
  const w = getTimerWorker();
  if (!w) {
    const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }
  const id = ++timerSeq;
  timerCallbacks.set(id, fn);
  w.postMessage({ id, ms });
  return () => timerCallbacks.delete(id);
}

export function sleep(ms, signal) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (signal?.aborted) return reject(signal.reason);
    const cancel = after(ms, () => {
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
      resolve();
    });
    const onAbort = () => {
      cancel();
      reject(signal.reason);
    };
    signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
  });
}

/** Resolves once the browser reports it is online again (immediately if it already is). */
export function waitForOnline(signal) {
  if (navigator.onLine) return Promise.resolve();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const done = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', done);
      signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
      resolve();
    };
    const onAbort = () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', done);
      reject(signal.reason);
    };
    window.addEventListener('online', done);
    signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });
  });
}

/** Files that operating systems scatter around and nobody wants on the NAS. */
const JUNK_FILES = new Set(['.ds_store', 'thumbs.db', 'ehthumbs.db', 'desktop.ini']);
const JUNK_DIRS = new Set(['$recycle.bin', 'system volume information', '.spotlight-v100', '.trashes', '.fseventsd']);

export function isJunkFile(name) {
  const n = name.toLowerCase();
  return JUNK_FILES.has(n) || n.startsWith('._');
}

export function isJunkDir(name) {
  return JUNK_DIRS.has(name.toLowerCase());
}

/** Names that can't be saved by Chrome's downloads API. */
export function sanitizeDownloadName(name) {
  const cleaned = name.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|\u0000-\u001f]/g, '_').replace(/^\.+/, '_').trim();
  return cleaned || 'download';
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/webdav.js (321 lines) =====
// Minimal WebDAV client built on XMLHttpRequest (XHR gives us upload progress, fetch does not).
// Works with Synology, QNAP, TrueNAS, Nextcloud, Apache mod_dav and any other RFC 4918 server.

import { normalizePath, encodePath, safeDecode, basicAuthHeader, parentPath, baseName } from './util.js';

const RETRYABLE_STATUS = new Set([0, 408, 423, 425, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504, 520, 521, 522, 523, 524]);

export class WebDAVError extends Error {
  constructor(message, { status = 0, retryable = false, aborted = false } = {}) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'WebDAVError';
    this.status = status;
    this.retryable = retryable;
    this.aborted = aborted;
  }
}

function describeStatus(status, method) {
  switch (status) {
    case 0:
      return 'Could not reach the NAS (network error, wrong address, or HTTPS certificate problem)';
    case 400:
      return 'The NAS rejected the request (HTTP 400) — the name may contain characters it does not allow';
    case 401:
      return 'The NAS rejected the username or password (HTTP 401)';
    case 403:
      return 'Permission denied by the NAS (HTTP 403) — this account cannot write here';
    case 404:
      return 'Not found on the NAS (HTTP 404)';
    case 405:
      return method === 'MKCOL' ? 'Folder already exists (HTTP 405)' : 'The NAS does not allow this operation here (HTTP 405)';
    case 409:
      return 'The parent folder does not exist on the NAS (HTTP 409)';
    case 413:
      return 'File is too large for the NAS or a proxy in front of it (HTTP 413) — raise its upload size limit';
    case 414:
      return 'Path is too long for the NAS (HTTP 414)';
    case 423:
      return 'The file is locked on the NAS (HTTP 423)';
    case 507:
      return 'The NAS is out of space (HTTP 507)';
    default:
      return `The NAS answered HTTP ${status}`;
  }
}

export class WebDAVClient {
  /**
   * @param {{serverUrl: string, username: string, password: string}} opts
   */
  constructor({ serverUrl, username, password }) {
    const url = new URL(serverUrl);
    if (!url.pathname.endsWith('/')) url.pathname += '/';
    url.search = '';
    url.hash = '';
    this.origin = url.origin;
    this.basePath = url.pathname; // already percent-encoded by URL
    this.decodedBasePath = safeDecode(url.pathname);
    this.username = username || '';
    this.password = password || '';
    this.authHeader = username || password ? basicAuthHeader(username, password) : null;
  }

  /** Absolute URL for a NAS path ("/" is the WebDAV root). Folders get a trailing slash when asked. */
  url(path, { dir = false } = {}) {
    const rel = encodePath(path).replace(/^\/+/, '');
    let u = this.origin + this.basePath + rel;
    if (dir && rel && !u.endsWith('/')) u += '/';
    return u;
  }

  /**
   * Low-level request. Resolves with {status, text, headers} for 2xx, throws WebDAVError otherwise.
   */
  request(method, path, { headers = {}, body = null, dir = false, timeout = 120000, signal, onUploadProgress, okStatuses, responseType = '' } = {}) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (signal?.aborted) {
        reject(new WebDAVError('Cancelled', { aborted: true }));
        return;
      }
      const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      // Passing the credentials here as well as in the header stops Chrome from showing (and waiting on)
      // its own login pop-up when the NAS answers 401; the request then simply fails with status 401.
      if (this.authHeader) xhr.open(method, this.url(path, { dir }), true, this.username, this.password);
      else xhr.open(method, this.url(path, { dir }), true);
      xhr.timeout = timeout;
      if (responseType) xhr.responseType = responseType;
      if (this.authHeader) xhr.setRequestHeader('Authorization', this.authHeader);
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(headers)) xhr.setRequestHeader(k, v);

      let stallTimer = null;
      let lastProgress = Date.now();
      const STALL_MS = 120000;
      const cleanup = () => {
        if (stallTimer) clearInterval(stallTimer);
        signal?.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);
      };
      const onAbort = () => xhr.abort();
      signal?.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });

      if (onUploadProgress && xhr.upload) {
        let sentAll = false;
        xhr.upload.onprogress = (e) => {
          lastProgress = Date.now();
          if (e.lengthComputable && e.loaded >= e.total) sentAll = true;
          onUploadProgress(e.loaded, e.total);
        };
        // Abort uploads that stop moving for two minutes so the retry logic can take over.
        stallTimer = setInterval(() => {
          if (!sentAll && Date.now() - lastProgress > STALL_MS) {
            xhr.stalled = true;
            xhr.abort();
          }
        }, 5000);
      }

      xhr.onload = () => {
        cleanup();
        const status = xhr.status;
        const ok = okStatuses ? okStatuses.includes(status) : status >= 200 && status < 300;
        if (ok) {
          const text = responseType && responseType !== 'text' ? '' : xhr.responseText;
          resolve({ status, text, response: xhr.response, headers: xhr.getAllResponseHeaders(), lastModified: xhr.getResponseHeader('Last-Modified') });
        } else {
          reject(new WebDAVError(describeStatus(status, method), { status, retryable: RETRYABLE_STATUS.has(status) }));
        }
      };
      xhr.onerror = () => {
        cleanup();
        reject(new WebDAVError(describeStatus(0, method), { status: 0, retryable: true }));
      };
      xhr.ontimeout = () => {
        cleanup();
        reject(new WebDAVError('The NAS took too long to answer', { status: 0, retryable: true }));
      };
      xhr.onabort = () => {
        cleanup();
        if (xhr.stalled) reject(new WebDAVError('Upload stalled (no progress for 2 minutes)', { status: 0, retryable: true }));
        else reject(new WebDAVError('Cancelled', { aborted: true }));
      };
      xhr.send(body);
    });
  }

  /** Convert an href from a PROPFIND response into a normalised NAS path, or null if it is outside our root. */
  hrefToPath(href) {
    let pathname;
    try {
      pathname = new URL(href, this.origin + this.basePath).pathname;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
    const decoded = safeDecode(pathname);
    if (decoded.startsWith(this.decodedBasePath)) return normalizePath(decoded.slice(this.decodedBasePath.length));
    if (decoded + '/' === this.decodedBasePath) return '/';
    return null;
  }

  /** Parse a multistatus body into entries. */
  parseMultistatus(xmlText, requestedPath) {
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(xmlText, 'application/xml');
    if (doc.getElementsByTagName('parsererror').length) {
      throw new WebDAVError('The NAS sent a folder listing that could not be read (is this really a WebDAV address?)');
    }
    const DAV = 'DAV:';
    const first = (el, name) => el.getElementsByTagNameNS(DAV, name)[0] || null;
    const entries = [];
    for (const resp of Array.from(doc.getElementsByTagNameNS(DAV, 'response'))) {
      const hrefEl = first(resp, 'href');
      if (!hrefEl) continue;
      const href = hrefEl.textContent.trim();
      let path = this.hrefToPath(href);
      const hrefIsDir = href.endsWith('/');

      // Collect properties from the propstat blocks that report success.
      let isDir = false;
      let size = null;
      let modified = null;
      let foundProps = false;
      for (const ps of Array.from(resp.getElementsByTagNameNS(DAV, 'propstat'))) {
        const statusEl = first(ps, 'status');
        if (statusEl && !/\s2\d\d\s/.test(' ' + statusEl.textContent + ' ')) continue;
        const prop = first(ps, 'prop');
        if (!prop) continue;
        foundProps = true;
        const rt = first(prop, 'resourcetype');
        if (rt && rt.getElementsByTagNameNS(DAV, 'collection').length) isDir = true;
        const len = first(prop, 'getcontentlength');
        if (len && len.textContent.trim() !== '') size = Number(len.textContent.trim());
        const lm = first(prop, 'getlastmodified');
        if (lm && lm.textContent.trim()) modified = new Date(lm.textContent.trim());
      }
      if (!foundProps) isDir = hrefIsDir;

      if (path === null) {
        // The server reports paths without our base prefix (e.g. a reverse proxy rewrote them).
        // Work out whether this entry is the requested folder itself or one of its children.
        let hp;
        try {
          hp = normalizePath(safeDecode(new URL(href, this.origin).pathname));
        } catch {
          continue;
        }
        const req = normalizePath(requestedPath);
        const hpParent = parentPath(hp);
        const isChild = hp !== req && (req === '/' ? hp !== '/' : hpParent === req || hpParent.endsWith(req));
        path = isChild ? normalizePath(req + '/' + baseName(hp)) : req;
      }
      entries.push({ path, name: baseName(path), isDir, size: isDir ? null : size, modified });
    }
    return entries;
  }

  async propfind(path, depth, { dir, ...opts } = {}) {
    const body =
      '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>' +
      '<d:propfind xmlns:d="DAV:"><d:prop><d:resourcetype/><d:getcontentlength/><d:getlastmodified/></d:prop></d:propfind>';
    const res = await this.request('PROPFIND', path, {
      headers: { Depth: String(depth), 'Content-Type': 'application/xml; charset=utf-8' },
      body,
      // Folders are always addressed with a trailing slash: otherwise many servers answer with a redirect,
      // which breaks behind HTTPS reverse proxies (the redirect points at http://).
      dir: dir ?? depth > 0,
      okStatuses: [207, 200],
      timeout: 300000, // listings of huge folders can be large
      ...opts,
    });
    return this.parseMultistatus(res.text, path);
  }

  /** List the immediate children of a folder. */
  async list(path, opts) {
    const target = normalizePath(path);
    const entries = await this.propfind(target, 1, opts);
    return entries.filter((e) => e.path !== target && e.name);
  }

  /** Info about a single path, or null if it does not exist. Pass {dir: true} when it should be a folder. */
  async stat(path, opts) {
    try {
      const entries = await this.propfind(normalizePath(path), 0, opts);
      return entries.find((e) => e.path === normalizePath(path)) || entries[0] || null;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 404) return null;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** Create one folder. Resolves to 'created' or 'exists'. Parent must exist. */
  async mkdir(path, opts) {
    const p = normalizePath(path);
    if (p === '/') return 'exists';
    try {
      await this.request('MKCOL', p, { dir: true, ...opts });
      return 'created';
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.aborted || err.retryable || err.status === 401 || err.status === 507) throw err;
      // 405 (exists) is the normal answer for an existing folder; some servers use 301/302/409/403. Check.
      const info = (await this.stat(p, { ...opts, dir: true }).catch(() => null)) || (await this.stat(p, opts).catch(() => null));
      if (info && info.isDir) return 'exists';
      if (info && !info.isDir) {
        throw new WebDAVError(`A file named "${baseName(p)}" already exists where a folder is needed`, { status: err.status });
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** Upload a File/Blob to path (overwrites). */
  upload(path, file, { onProgress, signal } = {}) {
    const headers = { 'Content-Type': 'application/octet-stream' };
    // Nextcloud/ownCloud keep the original modification time when given this header; other servers ignore it.
    if (file.lastModified) headers['X-OC-Mtime'] = String(Math.floor(file.lastModified / 1000));
    return this.request('PUT', path, { headers, body: file, timeout: 0, signal, onUploadProgress: onProgress });
  }

  async remove(path, { dir = false, ignoreMissing = false, ...opts } = {}) {
    const p = normalizePath(path);
    if (p === '/') throw new WebDAVError('Refusing to delete the root folder');
    try {
      await this.request('DELETE', p, { dir, ...opts });
    } catch (err) {
      if (ignoreMissing && err.status === 404) return;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** Server-side rename/move. Never overwrites: fails with 412 if the destination exists. */
  async move(from, to, { dir = false, ...opts } = {}) {
    const src = normalizePath(from);
    const dst = normalizePath(to);
    if (src === '/' || dst === '/') throw new WebDAVError('Refusing to move the root folder');
    await this.request('MOVE', src, { dir, headers: { Destination: this.url(dst, { dir }), Overwrite: 'F' }, ...opts });
  }

  /** Read a small text file; resolves to null if it does not exist. */
  async getText(path, opts) {
    try {
      const res = await this.request('GET', normalizePath(path), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }, ...opts });
      return res.text;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 404) return null;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  /** A file's contents as a File (null if it doesn't exist). */
  async getBlob(path, opts = {}) {
    try {
      const res = await this.request('GET', normalizePath(path), { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }, responseType: 'blob', timeout: 600000, ...opts });
      const lm = Date.parse(res.lastModified || '') || Date.now();
      return new File([res.response], normalizePath(path).split('/').pop(), { lastModified: lm, type: res.response?.type || '' });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err.status === 404) return null;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  putText(path, text, { contentType = 'application/json; charset=utf-8', ...opts } = {}) {
    return this.request('PUT', normalizePath(path), { headers: { 'Content-Type': contentType }, body: text, ...opts });
  }
}


===== FILE: extension/lib/whisper-worker.js (83 lines) =====
// Free speech-to-text on this computer: Whisper (transformers.js + ONNX Runtime WebAssembly) in a worker, so
// the meeting window stays responsive. The model is downloaded once (from Hugging Face, or from the NAS after
// the HP setup script copied it there) and kept in the browser's cache.

import { pipeline, env } from '../vendor/transformers.mjs';

env.allowLocalModels = false;
env.useBrowserCache = true;
env.useWasmCache = false; // the WebAssembly files ship inside the extension
env.backends.onnx.wasm.wasmPaths = new URL('../vendor/ort/', import.meta.url).href;
env.backends.onnx.wasm.proxy = false;

let asr = null;
let loadedKey = '';
let loading = null;

// What Whisper typically "hears" in near-silence or noise. Real short replies ("Okay.", "Thank you.") are kept.
const HALLUCINATION = /^[\s.!?]*(\[[^\]]*\]|\([^)]*\)|\u266a+|thanks for watching!?|you)[\s.!?]*$/i;

async function load({ model, host, pathTemplate, auth, device }) {
  const key = `${model}|${host}|${device}`;
  if (asr && loadedKey === key) return;
  if (loading && loadedKey === key) return loading;
  loadedKey = key;
  env.remoteHost = host.endsWith('/') ? host : `${host}/`;
  env.remotePathTemplate = pathTemplate || '{model}/resolve/{revision}/';
  // The NAS needs the login; Hugging Face must not get it.
  env.fetch = (url, init = {}) => {
    if (auth && String(url).startsWith(env.remoteHost)) {
      const headers = new Headers(init.headers || {});
      headers.set('Authorization', auth);
      return fetch(url, { ...init, headers });
    }
    return fetch(url, init);
  };
  loading = (async () => {
    const opts = {
      dtype: 'q8',
      progress_callback: (p) => {
        if (p.status === 'progress') postMessage({ type: 'progress', file: p.file, loaded: p.loaded, total: p.total });
      },
    };
    try {
      asr = await pipeline('automatic-speech-recognition', model, { ...opts, device: device || 'wasm' });
    } catch (err) {
      if (device && device !== 'wasm') asr = await pipeline('automatic-speech-recognition', model, { ...opts, device: 'wasm' }); // WebGPU unavailable
      else throw err;
    }
  })();
  try {
    await loading;
  } catch (err) {
    asr = null;
    loadedKey = '';
    throw err;
  } finally {
    loading = null;
  }
}

self.onmessage = async (e) => {
  const msg = e.data;
  try {
    if (msg.type === 'load') {
      await load(msg);
      postMessage({ type: 'loaded', id: msg.id });
    } else if (msg.type === 'transcribe') {
      if (!asr) await load(msg.load);
      const english = /\.en$/.test(msg.load.model);
      const t0 = performance.now();
      const out = await asr(msg.samples, {
        chunk_length_s: 30,
        return_timestamps: false,
        ...(english ? {} : { language: msg.language || 'english', task: 'transcribe' }),
      });
      let text = String(out?.text || '').trim();
      if (HALLUCINATION.test(text)) text = '';
      postMessage({ type: 'result', id: msg.id, text, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) });
    }
  } catch (err) {
    postMessage({ type: 'error', id: msg.id, error: String(err?.message || err) });
  }
};


===== FILE: extension/lib/zip.js (89 lines) =====
// Minimal ZIP reader for Blobs/Files (Office documents, archive listings).
// Reads only the central directory plus the entries asked for, so listing a
// multi-GB archive costs a few KB of I/O. Supports ZIP64 and deflate via the
// browser's native DecompressionStream.

const SIG_EOCD = 0x06054b50;
const SIG_Z64_LOC = 0x07064b50;
const SIG_Z64_EOCD = 0x06064b50;
const SIG_CEN = 0x02014b50;
const SIG_LOC = 0x04034b50;

async function readBytes(blob, start, end) {
  return new DataView(await blob.slice(start, end).arrayBuffer());
}

const u64 = (dv, o) => dv.getUint32(o, true) + dv.getUint32(o + 4, true) * 2 ** 32;

export async function listZip(blob) {
  const size = blob.size;
  const tailLen = Math.min(size, 65557 + 20);
  const tail = await readBytes(blob, size - tailLen, size);
  let eocd = -1;
  for (let i = tail.byteLength - 22; i >= 0; i--) {
    if (tail.getUint32(i, true) === SIG_EOCD) { eocd = i; break; }
  }
  if (eocd < 0) throw new Error('not a zip file');
  let total = tail.getUint16(eocd + 10, true);
  let cdSize = tail.getUint32(eocd + 12, true);
  let cdOff = tail.getUint32(eocd + 16, true);
  if (total === 0xffff || cdSize === 0xffffffff || cdOff === 0xffffffff) {
    const loc = eocd - 20;
    if (loc >= 0 && tail.getUint32(loc, true) === SIG_Z64_LOC) {
      const z64Off = u64(tail, loc + 8);
      const z = await readBytes(blob, z64Off, z64Off + 56);
      if (z.getUint32(0, true) !== SIG_Z64_EOCD) throw new Error('bad zip64 record');
      total = u64(z, 32);
      cdSize = u64(z, 40);
      cdOff = u64(z, 48);
    }
  }
  const cd = await readBytes(blob, cdOff, cdOff + cdSize);
  const dec = new TextDecoder();
  const entries = [];
  let p = 0;
  for (let n = 0; n < total && p + 46 <= cd.byteLength; n++) {
    if (cd.getUint32(p, true) !== SIG_CEN) break;
    const method = cd.getUint16(p + 10, true);
    let csize = cd.getUint32(p + 20, true);
    let usize = cd.getUint32(p + 24, true);
    const nameLen = cd.getUint16(p + 28, true);
    const extraLen = cd.getUint16(p + 30, true);
    const commentLen = cd.getUint16(p + 32, true);
    let offset = cd.getUint32(p + 42, true);
    const name = dec.decode(new Uint8Array(cd.buffer, cd.byteOffset + p + 46, nameLen));
    // ZIP64 extra field holds the real values for any field set to 0xFFFFFFFF.
    let e = p + 46 + nameLen;
    const eEnd = e + extraLen;
    while (e + 4 <= eEnd) {
      const id = cd.getUint16(e, true);
      const len = cd.getUint16(e + 2, true);
      if (id === 0x0001) {
        let q = e + 4;
        if (usize === 0xffffffff) { usize = u64(cd, q); q += 8; }
        if (csize === 0xffffffff) { csize = u64(cd, q); q += 8; }
        if (offset === 0xffffffff) { offset = u64(cd, q); q += 8; }
      }
      e += 4 + len;
    }
    entries.push({ name, method, csize, usize, offset, dir: name.endsWith('/') });
    p += 46 + nameLen + extraLen + commentLen;
  }
  return entries;
}

export async function readEntry(blob, entry, maxBytes = 64 * 1024 * 1024) {
  if (entry.usize > maxBytes) throw new Error(`zip entry too large: ${entry.name}`);
  const h = await readBytes(blob, entry.offset, entry.offset + 30);
  if (h.getUint32(0, true) !== SIG_LOC) throw new Error('bad local header');
  const start = entry.offset + 30 + h.getUint16(26, true) + h.getUint16(28, true);
  const data = blob.slice(start, start + entry.csize);
  if (entry.method === 0) return new Uint8Array(await data.arrayBuffer());
  if (entry.method !== 8) throw new Error(`unsupported zip method ${entry.method}`);
  const stream = data.stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw'));
  return new Uint8Array(await new Response(stream).arrayBuffer());
}

export async function readEntryText(blob, entry) {
  return new TextDecoder().decode(await readEntry(blob, entry));
}


===== FILE: server/setup-webdav.sh (726 lines) =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
#  NAS Uploader — server setup for a Proxmox LXC container (Debian / Ubuntu)
# =============================================================================
#  Run this as root INSIDE the container that holds your media — the one
#  Jellyfin reads its libraries from (Proxmox web UI → the container → Console).
#
#  It installs a WebDAV server (Apache) for the folder Jellyfin uses, makes sure
#  uploads stay readable by Jellyfin, makes it reachable from outside your home,
#  and prints the exact settings to paste into the extension's config.js.
#
#    bash setup-webdav.sh                       # asks a few questions
#    bash setup-webdav.sh --access funnel       # Tailscale Funnel: no router changes
#    bash setup-webdav.sh --access port-forward --domain NAME.duckdns.org \
#         --duckdns-token TOKEN --folder /mnt/mirror
#                                               # router: forward TCP 443 to this container
#    bash setup-webdav.sh --access local        # home network only (self-signed HTTPS)
#    bash setup-webdav.sh --uninstall           # remove (your files are never touched)
#
#  Other options:
#    --folder PATH        folder to share, e.g. /mnt/mirror for a drive added just for this
#                         (default: offers Jellyfin's library folder and other mounted drives)
#    --user NAME          WebDAV username (default: uploader)
#    --password PASS      password (default: keep the existing one, or generate one)
#    --port N             HTTPS port for local/port-forward (default 443)
#    --access proxy --public-url https://files.example.com/
#                         behind your own reverse proxy (Nginx Proxy Manager, Caddy…)
#    --local-port N       plain-HTTP port Apache uses behind Funnel / a proxy (default 8090)
#    --email ADDR         optional, given to Let's Encrypt
#    --acme-server URL    certificate authority (default Let's Encrypt); e.g. Let's Encrypt staging for a dry run
#    --yes                don't ask questions; use defaults
#    --no-ocr             don't set up the OCR job for scanned PDFs (Project Assistant's "deep index")
#    --no-whisper         don't copy the free speech-to-text model to the NAS
#
#  Safe to run again (e.g. to change the password or the folder).
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail

FOLDER=""
WEB_USER="uploader"
WEB_PASS=""
ACCESS=""
DOMAIN=""
EMAIL=""
DUCKDNS_TOKEN=""
PORT="443"
LOCAL_PORT="8090"
PUBLIC_URL=""
ASSUME_YES=0
UNINSTALL=0
SKIP_TAILSCALE=0 # for testing the Apache side of Funnel mode without Tailscale
ACME_SERVER=""
NO_OCR=0
NO_WHISPER=0
# Overridable for automated tests only.
DUCKDNS_URL="${NAS_UPLOADER_DUCKDNS_URL:-https://www.duckdns.org}"
DNS_WAIT="${NAS_UPLOADER_DNS_WAIT:-60}"
HF_URL="${NAS_UPLOADER_HF_URL:-https://huggingface.co}"
RAW_BASE="${NAS_UPLOADER_RAW_BASE:-https://raw.githubusercontent.com/montgomeryburke/Receipts/claude/friendly-pasteur-uixwht/nas-uploader/server}"
ACME_ROOT=/var/lib/nas-uploader/acme

SITE=/etc/apache2/sites-available/nas-uploader.conf
HTPASSWD=/etc/apache2/nas-uploader.htpasswd
STATE_DIR=/etc/nas-uploader
LOCK_DIR=/var/lib/nas-uploader
CERT_DIR=/etc/ssl/nas-uploader
SUMMARY=/root/nas-uploader-settings.txt

say()  { printf '\033[1;36m==>\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
ok()   { printf '\033[1;32m ✓\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
warn() { printf '\033[1;33m !\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
die()  { printf '\033[1;31m ✗ %s\033[0m\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }

usage() {
  if [[ -f "$0" ]]; then sed -n '2,35p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,2\}//'; else echo "See the comments at the top of setup-webdav.sh for options."; fi
  exit "${1:-0}"
}

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    --folder) FOLDER="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --user) WEB_USER="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --password) WEB_PASS="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --access) ACCESS="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --domain) DOMAIN="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --email) EMAIL="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --duckdns-token) DUCKDNS_TOKEN="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --port) PORT="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --local-port) LOCAL_PORT="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --public-url) PUBLIC_URL="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --yes|-y) ASSUME_YES=1; shift ;;
    --uninstall) UNINSTALL=1; shift ;;
    --skip-tailscale) SKIP_TAILSCALE=1; shift ;;
    --acme-server) ACME_SERVER="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --no-ocr) NO_OCR=1; shift ;;
    --no-whisper) NO_WHISPER=1; shift ;;
    -h|--help) usage 0 ;;
    *) echo "Unknown option: $1" >&2; usage 2 ;;
  esac
done

[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "Run this as root (in the Proxmox container console you already are root)."
command -v apt-get >/dev/null || die "This script supports Debian/Ubuntu containers (apt-get not found)."

ask() { # ask "question" default -> echoes answer
  local q="$1" def="${2:-}" a
  if [[ $ASSUME_YES -eq 1 ]]; then echo "$def"; return; fi
  read -r -p "$q${def:+ [$def]}: " a </dev/tty || true
  echo "${a:-$def}"
}
confirm() { # confirm "question" -> 0 if yes
  [[ $ASSUME_YES -eq 1 ]] && return 0
  local a; read -r -p "$1 [Y/n]: " a </dev/tty || true
  [[ -z "$a" || "$a" =~ ^[Yy] ]]
}

have_systemd() { [[ -d /run/systemd/system ]]; }
apache_reload() {
  apache2ctl configtest >/dev/null 2>&1 || { apache2ctl configtest; die "Apache configuration test failed (see above)."; }
  if have_systemd; then
    systemctl enable apache2 >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    systemctl restart apache2
  else
    apache2ctl -k stop >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    sleep 1
    apache2ctl -k start
  fi
}

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- uninstall
if [[ $UNINSTALL -eq 1 ]]; then
  say "Removing the NAS Uploader server (your files are not touched)"
  a2dissite nas-uploader >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  if have_systemd; then systemctl disable --now nas-uploader-ocr.timer >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; fi
  rm -f /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-ocr.service /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-ocr.timer /etc/cron.d/nas-uploader-ocr /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py
  rm -f "$SITE" "$HTPASSWD" /etc/cron.d/nas-uploader-duckdns /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-duckdns \
    /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-auth /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-cleanup /etc/fail2ban/jail.d/nas-uploader.conf
  if [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/envvars.backup" ]]; then cp "$STATE_DIR/envvars.backup" /etc/apache2/envvars; fi
  if command -v tailscale >/dev/null && [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/funnel" ]]; then
    tailscale funnel --https=443 off >/dev/null 2>&1 || tailscale funnel reset >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  fi
  rm -rf "$STATE_DIR"
  apache_reload || true
  ok "Removed. Apache itself is still installed (apt-get remove apache2 to remove it too)."
  exit 0
fi

mkdir -p "$STATE_DIR"
chmod 700 "$STATE_DIR"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 1. which folder
say "Looking for the folder Jellyfin uses"
declare -a CANDIDATES=()
JELLYFIN_HERE=0
if [[ -d /var/lib/jellyfin ]] || id jellyfin >/dev/null 2>&1; then JELLYFIN_HERE=1; fi

common_parent() { # common_parent path... -> longest shared directory
  local first="$1"; shift
  local prefix="$first"
  for p in "$@"; do
    while [[ "$p/" != "$prefix/"* ]]; do prefix="${prefix%/*}"; [[ -z "$prefix" ]] && { echo "/"; return; }; done
  done
  echo "$prefix"
}

declare -a LIBS=()
if [[ -d /var/lib/jellyfin/root/default ]]; then
  while IFS= read -r p; do [[ -n "$p" && -d "$p" ]] && LIBS+=("$p"); done < <(
    { grep -ho '<Path>[^<]*</Path>' /var/lib/jellyfin/root/default/*/options.xml 2>/dev/null | sed 's/<\/\{0,1\}Path>//g'
      cat /var/lib/jellyfin/root/default/*/*.mblink 2>/dev/null; echo; } | sed '/^$/d' | sort -u)
fi
if [[ ${#LIBS[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then
  ok "Jellyfin libraries in this container: ${LIBS[*]}"
  parent="$(common_parent "${LIBS[@]}")"
  if [[ ${#LIBS[@]} -gt 1 && "$parent" != "/" ]]; then CANDIDATES+=("$parent"); fi
  CANDIDATES+=("${LIBS[@]}")
fi
# Proxmox mount points (mp0, mp1, …) show up as mounts; typical media locations too.
while IFS= read -r m; do
  [[ -d "$m" ]] && CANDIDATES+=("$m")
done < <(awk '$2 ~ "^/(mnt|media|srv|data|storage|tank)(/|$)" {print $2}' /proc/mounts 2>/dev/null | sort -u)
for d in /mnt/media /media /srv/media /data /mnt/data /mnt/storage; do [[ -d "$d" ]] && CANDIDATES+=("$d"); done
# de-duplicate, keep order
declare -A seen=(); declare -a UNIQUE=()
for c in "${CANDIDATES[@]}"; do [[ -z "${seen[$c]:-}" ]] && { seen[$c]=1; UNIQUE+=("$c"); }; done

if [[ -z "$FOLDER" ]]; then
  if [[ ${#UNIQUE[@]} -eq 0 ]]; then
    [[ $ASSUME_YES -eq 1 ]] && die "Couldn't find a media folder. Run again with --folder /path/to/media"
    FOLDER="$(ask 'Which folder should the uploader write into? (full path)')"
  else
    echo "Folders found:"
    for i in "${!UNIQUE[@]}"; do echo "  $((i + 1))) ${UNIQUE[$i]}"; done
    choice="$(ask 'Number of the folder to share, or type a path' 1)"
    if [[ "$choice" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && (( choice >= 1 && choice <= ${#UNIQUE[@]} )); then FOLDER="${UNIQUE[$((choice - 1))]}"; else FOLDER="$choice"; fi
  fi
fi
FOLDER="$(realpath -m "$FOLDER")"
[[ -d "$FOLDER" ]] || die "$FOLDER does not exist (or isn't a folder) in this container."
[[ "$FOLDER" != "/" && "$FOLDER" != "/etc" && "$FOLDER" != "/root" && "$FOLDER" != "/var" ]] || die "Refusing to share $FOLDER."
ok "Sharing: $FOLDER"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 2. install
say "Installing Apache with WebDAV"
export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
FRESH_APACHE=0
dpkg -s apache2 >/dev/null 2>&1 || FRESH_APACHE=1
PKGS=(apache2 apache2-utils openssl curl ca-certificates acl)
missing=()
for p in "${PKGS[@]}"; do dpkg -s "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 || missing+=("$p"); done
if [[ ${#missing[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then
  apt-get update -qq
  apt-get install -y -qq "${missing[@]}" >/dev/null
fi
# Security fixes for Apache (and the rest of this container) install automatically.
if dpkg -s unattended-upgrades >/dev/null 2>&1 || apt-get install -y -qq unattended-upgrades >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  printf 'APT::Periodic::Update-Package-Lists "1";\nAPT::Periodic::Unattended-Upgrade "1";\n' > /etc/apt/apt.conf.d/52nas-uploader-auto-upgrades
  ok "Security updates will install automatically"
else
  warn "Couldn't set up automatic security updates; run 'apt-get upgrade' now and then."
fi
a2enmod -q dav dav_fs auth_basic authn_file headers ssl >/dev/null
# Silence Apache's "could not determine the server's fully qualified domain name" notice.
if ! grep -rqsE '^\s*ServerName\s' /etc/apache2/apache2.conf /etc/apache2/conf-enabled/; then
  echo 'ServerName localhost' > /etc/apache2/conf-available/nas-uploader-servername.conf
  a2enconf -q nas-uploader-servername >/dev/null
fi
# A freshly installed Apache only serves Debian's placeholder page; switch that off.
if [[ $FRESH_APACHE -eq 1 ]]; then a2dissite -q 000-default >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; fi
ok "Apache installed"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 3. permissions
say "Checking that uploads can be written to $FOLDER"
owner_uid="$(stat -c %u "$FOLDER")"
owner="$(stat -c %U "$FOLDER")"
group="$(stat -c %G "$FOLDER")"
if [[ "$owner_uid" == "65534" || "$owner" == "nobody" ]]; then
  cat >&2 <<EOF

  ✗ Inside this container, $FOLDER belongs to "nobody".

  That happens with an *unprivileged* Proxmox container whose media folder is owned on
  the Proxmox host by a user the container can't see, so nothing in here can write to it.
  Fix it once on the Proxmox HOST (not in this container), then run this script again:

    1. Find the host path of the folder:      pct config <CTID> | grep mp
    2. Give it to this container's users:      chown -R 100000:100000 /host/path/to/media
       (100000 = root inside an unprivileged container; Jellyfin and this uploader can then
        both read it, and this script will grant write access)

EOF
  exit 1
fi

RUN_USER="www-data"
RUN_GROUP="www-data"
as_user() { setpriv --reuid="$1" --regid="$(id -g "$1")" --init-groups -- "${@:2}"; }
can_write() { as_user "$1" sh -c "touch '$FOLDER/.nas-uploader-test' && rm -f '$FOLDER/.nas-uploader-test'" 2>/dev/null; }

if [[ "$owner_uid" != "0" ]] && id "$owner" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  # Run the WebDAV server as the folder's owner, so uploads look exactly like the files already there.
  RUN_USER="$owner"
  RUN_GROUP="$group"
  can_write "$RUN_USER" || die "$owner owns $FOLDER but can't write to it (check its permissions: ls -ld '$FOLDER')."
  ok "Uploads will be owned by $owner:$group, like the existing files"
elif can_write www-data; then
  ok "The web server can already write here"
else
  # Root-owned media folder: give the web server write access to the folders (not the files) with ACLs.
  say "Granting the web server write access to the folders in $FOLDER (file contents are not changed)"
  if find "$FOLDER" -type d -exec setfacl -m u:www-data:rwx -m d:u:www-data:rwx {} + 2>/dev/null && can_write www-data; then
    ok "Write access granted with ACLs"
  else
    warn "This filesystem doesn't support ACLs (common for ZFS datasets with acltype=off)."
    if confirm "Make the group 'www-data' co-own the FOLDERS in $FOLDER (chgrp + group write; files untouched)?"; then
      find "$FOLDER" -type d -exec chgrp www-data {} + -exec chmod g+rwxs {} +
      can_write www-data || die "Still can't write to $FOLDER. Check its permissions on the Proxmox host."
      ok "Write access granted via the www-data group"
    else
      die "No write access to $FOLDER. Re-run when you're ready, or pick another folder with --folder."
    fi
  fi
fi

if [[ ! -f "$STATE_DIR/envvars.backup" ]]; then cp /etc/apache2/envvars "$STATE_DIR/envvars.backup"; fi
sed -i -E "s/^export APACHE_RUN_USER=.*/export APACHE_RUN_USER=$RUN_USER/; s/^export APACHE_RUN_GROUP=.*/export APACHE_RUN_GROUP=$RUN_GROUP/" /etc/apache2/envvars
mkdir -p "$LOCK_DIR" /var/lock/apache2
# The WebDAV lock database only holds short-lived locks; start it fresh if the server's user changed.
if [[ "$(cat "$STATE_DIR/run-user" 2>/dev/null)" != "$RUN_USER" ]]; then rm -f "$LOCK_DIR"/DavLock*; fi
echo "$RUN_USER" > "$STATE_DIR/run-user"
echo "$FOLDER" > "$STATE_DIR/folder"
chown -R "$RUN_USER:$RUN_GROUP" "$LOCK_DIR" /var/lock/apache2

# Subfolders the web server can't write to (e.g. owned by someone else) are worth knowing about.
unwritable=()
while IFS= read -r -d '' d; do
  as_user "$RUN_USER" test -w "$d" 2>/dev/null || unwritable+=("$d")
done < <(find "$FOLDER" -mindepth 1 -maxdepth 1 -type d ! -name lost+found -print0 2>/dev/null)
if [[ ${#unwritable[@]} -gt 0 ]]; then warn "Not writable by the uploader (uploads into these will fail): ${unwritable[*]}"; fi

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 4. credentials
say "Setting up the login"
if [[ -z "$WEB_PASS" && -f "$HTPASSWD" ]] && grep -q "^$WEB_USER:" "$HTPASSWD" && [[ -f "$STATE_DIR/password" ]]; then
  WEB_PASS="$(cat "$STATE_DIR/password")"
  ok "Keeping the existing password for $WEB_USER"
else
  if [[ -z "$WEB_PASS" ]]; then
    WEB_PASS="$(tr -dc 'A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z2-9' </dev/urandom | head -c 20)"
  fi
  htpasswd -iBc "$HTPASSWD" "$WEB_USER" <<<"$WEB_PASS" >/dev/null 2>&1
  printf '%s' "$WEB_PASS" > "$STATE_DIR/password"
  ok "User $WEB_USER created"
fi
chown "root:$RUN_GROUP" "$HTPASSWD"
chmod 640 "$HTPASSWD"
chmod 600 "$STATE_DIR/password"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 5. how to reach it
if [[ -z "$ACCESS" ]]; then
  if [[ -n "$PUBLIC_URL" ]]; then ACCESS=proxy
  elif [[ $ASSUME_YES -eq 1 ]]; then ACCESS=local
  else
    cat <<'EOF'

How should the offsite computer reach this? (With every choice, the offsite computer
needs nothing but Chrome and the extension. Nothing gets installed there.)
  1) Port forward on your router + free DuckDNS name + Let's Encrypt certificate.
     Fastest, best for very large mirrors. Needs access to your router (ports 443 and 80).
  2) Tailscale Funnel: Tailscale runs on THIS machine only and publishes a normal
     https:// address. No router changes. Tailscale limits the speed somewhat.
  3) Home network only for now (self-signed certificate). You can re-run later.
EOF
    case "$(ask 'Choose 1, 2 or 3' 1)" in
      1) ACCESS=port-forward ;;
      2) ACCESS=funnel ;;
      *) ACCESS=local ;;
    esac
  fi
fi
case "$ACCESS" in funnel|port-forward|local|proxy) ;; *) die "--access must be funnel, port-forward, local or proxy";; esac

LAN_IP="$(hostname -I 2>/dev/null | awk '{print $1}')"
LAN_IP="${LAN_IP:-127.0.0.1}"
CERT=""
KEY=""

self_signed() {
  mkdir -p "$CERT_DIR"
  local cn="${DOMAIN:-$LAN_IP}"
  if [[ ! -f "$CERT_DIR/cert.pem" ]] || ! openssl x509 -in "$CERT_DIR/cert.pem" -noout -text | grep -q "$cn"; then
    openssl req -x509 -newkey rsa:2048 -nodes -days 3650 -subj "/CN=$cn" \
      -addext "subjectAltName=DNS:${DOMAIN:-localhost},DNS:localhost,IP:$LAN_IP,IP:127.0.0.1" \
      -addext "basicConstraints=critical,CA:FALSE" -addext "extendedKeyUsage=serverAuth" \
      -keyout "$CERT_DIR/key.pem" -out "$CERT_DIR/cert.pem" >/dev/null 2>&1
    chmod 600 "$CERT_DIR/key.pem"
  fi
  CERT="$CERT_DIR/cert.pem"
  KEY="$CERT_DIR/key.pem"
}

if [[ "$ACCESS" == "funnel" ]]; then
  if [[ $SKIP_TAILSCALE -eq 0 ]]; then
    say "Setting up Tailscale Funnel"
    if ! command -v tailscale >/dev/null; then
      curl -fsSL https://tailscale.com/install.sh | sh
    fi
    if [[ ! -c /dev/net/tun ]]; then
      # Unprivileged containers usually have no TUN device; Funnel works fine in userspace mode.
      warn "No /dev/net/tun in this container: running Tailscale in userspace mode (fine for Funnel)."
      if [[ -f /etc/default/tailscaled ]] && ! grep -q 'userspace-networking' /etc/default/tailscaled; then
        sed -i -E 's/^FLAGS="(.*)"/FLAGS="\1 --tun=userspace-networking"/' /etc/default/tailscaled
      fi
    fi
    if have_systemd; then systemctl enable --now tailscaled >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; systemctl restart tailscaled; fi
    if ! tailscale status >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      say "Sign this container in to your Tailscale account: open the link below on any device."
