      tailscale up
    fi
    TS_NAME="$(tailscale status --json --peers=false 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/.*"DNSName": *"\([^"]*\)".*/\1/p' | head -1)"
    TS_NAME="${TS_NAME%.}"
    [[ -n "$TS_NAME" ]] || die "Couldn't read this machine's Tailscale name (tailscale status)."
    PUBLIC_URL="https://$TS_NAME/"
  else
    [[ -n "$PUBLIC_URL" ]] || die "--skip-tailscale needs --public-url"
  fi
elif [[ "$ACCESS" == "port-forward" ]]; then
  [[ -n "$DOMAIN" ]] || DOMAIN="$(ask 'Public name for your home (e.g. myhome.duckdns.org)')"
  [[ -n "$DOMAIN" ]] || die "port-forward needs a name (--domain). Get a free one at https://www.duckdns.org"
  if [[ "$DOMAIN" == *.duckdns.org && -z "$DUCKDNS_TOKEN" ]]; then
    DUCKDNS_TOKEN="$(ask 'DuckDNS token (from duckdns.org, keeps the name pointing at your home; Enter to skip)')"
  fi
  if [[ -z "$DUCKDNS_TOKEN" && -f "$STATE_DIR/duckdns.env" ]] && grep -qx "DOMAIN='${DOMAIN%.duckdns.org}'" "$STATE_DIR/duckdns.env"; then
    DUCKDNS_TOKEN="$(sed -n "s/^TOKEN='\(.*\)'$/\1/p" "$STATE_DIR/duckdns.env")" # keep the one from an earlier run
  fi
  if [[ -n "$DUCKDNS_TOKEN" ]]; then
    [[ "$DOMAIN" == *.duckdns.org ]] || die "--duckdns-token only works with a NAME.duckdns.org name"
    [[ "$DUCKDNS_TOKEN" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9-]+$ ]] || die "That doesn't look like a DuckDNS token (letters, digits and dashes)."
    say "Keeping $DOMAIN pointed at your home's internet address (DuckDNS, every 5 minutes)"
    sub="${DOMAIN%.duckdns.org}"
    ( umask 077
      printf "DOMAIN='%s'\nTOKEN='%s'\nURL='%s'\nWAIT='%s'\n" "$sub" "$DUCKDNS_TOKEN" "$DUCKDNS_URL" "$DNS_WAIT" > "$STATE_DIR/duckdns.env" )
    cat > /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-duckdns <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Points NAME.duckdns.org at this home's current internet address.
. /etc/nas-uploader/duckdns.env
curl -fsS --max-time 30 "$URL/update?domains=$DOMAIN&token=$TOKEN&ip="
EOF
    # Let's Encrypt "DNS" check through DuckDNS: proves the name is ours without needing port 80.
    cat > /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-auth <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
. /etc/nas-uploader/duckdns.env
r="$(curl -fsS --max-time 30 "$URL/update?domains=$DOMAIN&token=$TOKEN&txt=$CERTBOT_VALIDATION")" || exit 1
case "$r" in OK*) ;; *) echo "DuckDNS refused the update ($r). Check the token." >&2; exit 1 ;; esac
sleep "$WAIT"
EOF
    cat > /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-cleanup <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
. /etc/nas-uploader/duckdns.env
curl -fsS --max-time 30 "$URL/update?domains=$DOMAIN&token=$TOKEN&txt=removed&clear=true" >/dev/null || true
EOF
    chmod 700 /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-duckdns /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-auth /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-cleanup
    echo '*/5 * * * * root /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-duckdns >/dev/null 2>&1' > /etc/cron.d/nas-uploader-duckdns
    if [[ "$(/usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-duckdns 2>/dev/null || true)" == OK* ]]; then ok "DuckDNS updated"; else warn "DuckDNS update failed. Check the name and token at duckdns.org."; fi
  fi
  PUBLIC_URL="https://$DOMAIN$([[ "$PORT" != 443 ]] && echo ":$PORT")/"
elif [[ "$ACCESS" == "proxy" ]]; then
  [[ -n "$PUBLIC_URL" ]] || die "--access proxy needs --public-url https://your.address/"
else
  PUBLIC_URL="https://$LAN_IP$([[ "$PORT" != 443 ]] && echo ":$PORT")/"
fi
PUBLIC_URL="${PUBLIC_URL%/}/"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 6. Apache site
say "Writing the web server configuration"
COMMON=$(cat <<EOF
    DocumentRoot "$FOLDER"
    DavLockDB "$LOCK_DIR/DavLock"
    # No upload size limit (Apache 2.4.54+ otherwise stops at 1 GB), and patience for slow links.
    LimitRequestBody 0
    Timeout 600
    <Directory "$FOLDER">
        Dav On
        Options None
        AllowOverride None
        <IfModule mod_dir.c>
            DirectorySlash Off
        </IfModule>
        AuthType Basic
        AuthName "NAS Uploader"
        AuthBasicProvider file
        AuthUserFile "$HTPASSWD"
        Require valid-user
        # Never run anything that gets uploaded.
        <IfModule mod_mime.c>
            RemoveHandler .php .phtml .cgi .pl .py .sh
        </IfModule>
        <IfModule php_module>
            php_admin_flag engine off
        </IfModule>
        <IfModule php7_module>
            php_admin_flag engine off
        </IfModule>
    </Directory>
    ErrorLog \${APACHE_LOG_DIR}/nas-uploader-error.log
    CustomLog \${APACHE_LOG_DIR}/nas-uploader-access.log combined
EOF
)

if [[ "$ACCESS" == "funnel" || "$ACCESS" == "proxy" ]]; then
  # Behind Tailscale / a reverse proxy, Apache speaks plain HTTP on localhost. Telling it its public https://
  # address keeps redirects and WebDAV renames (MOVE) working.
  pu="${PUBLIC_URL%/}"
  pu_host="${pu#https://}"; pu_host="${pu_host#http://}"
  [[ "$pu_host" == *:* ]] || pu_host="$pu_host:$([[ "$pu" == https://* ]] && echo 443 || echo 80)"
  scheme="${pu%%://*}"
  LISTEN_ADDR="127.0.0.1:$LOCAL_PORT"
  [[ "$ACCESS" == "proxy" ]] && LISTEN_ADDR="0.0.0.0:$LOCAL_PORT"
  cat > "$SITE" <<EOF
# Managed by NAS Uploader setup-webdav.sh. Re-run the script instead of editing.
Listen $LISTEN_ADDR
<VirtualHost $LISTEN_ADDR>
    ServerName $scheme://$pu_host
    UseCanonicalName On
$COMMON
</VirtualHost>
EOF
else
  write_tls_site() { # write_tls_site CERT KEY
    local listen_line="" http_vhost=""
    grep -qE "^\s*Listen\s+$PORT\b" /etc/apache2/ports.conf || listen_line="Listen $PORT"
    if [[ "$ACCESS" == "port-forward" ]]; then
      # Port 80 answers Let's Encrypt's checks (when the DNS method isn't used) and sends everything else to HTTPS.
      a2dissite -q 000-default >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
      mkdir -p "$ACME_ROOT/.well-known/acme-challenge"
      http_vhost="<VirtualHost *:80>
    ServerName $DOMAIN
    Alias /.well-known/acme-challenge/ \"$ACME_ROOT/.well-known/acme-challenge/\"
    <Directory \"$ACME_ROOT\">
        Options None
        AllowOverride None
        Require all granted
    </Directory>
    RedirectMatch 301 ^/(?!\.well-known/acme-challenge/)(.*)\$ ${PUBLIC_URL}\$1
</VirtualHost>"
    fi
    cat > "$SITE" <<EOF
# Managed by NAS Uploader setup-webdav.sh. Re-run the script instead of editing.
$listen_line
$http_vhost
<VirtualHost *:$PORT>
    ServerName ${DOMAIN:-$LAN_IP}
    ServerAlias localhost $LAN_IP
    SSLEngine on
    SSLCertificateFile "$1"
    SSLCertificateKeyFile "$2"
$COMMON
</VirtualHost>
EOF
    a2ensite -q nas-uploader >/dev/null
    apache_reload
  }

  LE_LIVE="/etc/letsencrypt/live/$DOMAIN"
  if [[ "$ACCESS" == "port-forward" && -n "$DOMAIN" && -f "$LE_LIVE/fullchain.pem" ]]; then
    CERT="$LE_LIVE/fullchain.pem"; KEY="$LE_LIVE/privkey.pem"
    write_tls_site "$CERT" "$KEY"
    ok "Using the Let's Encrypt certificate from an earlier run (it renews automatically)"
  else
    self_signed # also the stand-in while Let's Encrypt checks the name
    write_tls_site "$CERT" "$KEY"
    if [[ "$ACCESS" == "port-forward" ]]; then
      command -v certbot >/dev/null || apt-get install -y -qq certbot >/dev/null
      args=(certonly --non-interactive --agree-tos --cert-name "$DOMAIN" -d "$DOMAIN" --deploy-hook "apache2ctl -k graceful")
      if [[ -n "$EMAIL" ]]; then args+=(-m "$EMAIL"); else args+=(--register-unsafely-without-email); fi
      [[ -n "$ACME_SERVER" ]] && args+=(--server "$ACME_SERVER")
      if [[ -n "$DUCKDNS_TOKEN" ]]; then
        say "Getting a free Let's Encrypt certificate for $DOMAIN (checked through DuckDNS, no port 80 needed; about a minute)"
        args+=(--manual --preferred-challenges dns --manual-auth-hook /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-auth --manual-cleanup-hook /usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-dns-cleanup)
        hint="Check the DuckDNS name and token at duckdns.org."
      else
        say "Getting a free Let's Encrypt certificate for $DOMAIN (needs TCP port 80 forwarded to $LAN_IP)"
        args+=(--webroot -w "$ACME_ROOT")
        hint="Is TCP port 80 forwarded from your router to $LAN_IP, and does $DOMAIN point at your home?"
      fi
      if certbot "${args[@]}" >"$STATE_DIR/certbot.log" 2>&1; then
        CERT="$LE_LIVE/fullchain.pem"; KEY="$LE_LIVE/privkey.pem"
        write_tls_site "$CERT" "$KEY"
        ok "Certificate issued (renews automatically)"
      else
        warn "Let's Encrypt didn't issue a certificate. $hint"
        warn "Details: $STATE_DIR/certbot.log. Using a self-signed certificate for now; run this script again to retry."
      fi
    fi
  fi
fi
[[ "$ACCESS" == "funnel" || "$ACCESS" == "proxy" ]] && { a2ensite -q nas-uploader >/dev/null; apache_reload; }
ok "Web server running"

if [[ "$ACCESS" == "funnel" && $SKIP_TAILSCALE -eq 0 ]]; then
  say "Publishing it on $PUBLIC_URL"
  tailscale funnel --bg --https=443 "http://127.0.0.1:$LOCAL_PORT" || die "Funnel didn't start. If it printed a link, open it to allow Funnel for this machine, then run this script again."
  touch "$STATE_DIR/funnel"
fi

# Brute-force protection when the server is directly on the internet.
if [[ "$ACCESS" == "port-forward" ]]; then
  if dpkg -s fail2ban >/dev/null 2>&1 || apt-get install -y -qq fail2ban >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    cat > /etc/fail2ban/jail.d/nas-uploader.conf <<'EOF'
[apache-auth]
enabled  = true
port     = http,https
logpath  = /var/log/apache2/nas-uploader-error.log
maxretry = 10
findtime = 10m
bantime  = 1h
EOF
    if fail2ban-client -t >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      { have_systemd && systemctl restart fail2ban; } || true
      ok "Failed logins are blocked after 10 tries (fail2ban)"
    else
      rm -f /etc/fail2ban/jail.d/nas-uploader.conf
      warn "fail2ban configuration test failed; skipped."
    fi
  fi
fi

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 7. self-test
say "Testing"
if [[ "$ACCESS" == "funnel" || "$ACCESS" == "proxy" ]]; then TEST_URL="http://127.0.0.1:$LOCAL_PORT/"; else TEST_URL="https://127.0.0.1:$PORT/"; fi
code="$(curl -sk -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -X PROPFIND -H 'Depth: 0' -u "$WEB_USER:$WEB_PASS" "$TEST_URL")"
[[ "$code" == 207 ]] || die "The server answered $code instead of 207. See /var/log/apache2/nas-uploader-error.log"
probe=".nas-uploader-selftest-$$.txt"
code="$(curl -sk -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -T - -u "$WEB_USER:$WEB_PASS" "$TEST_URL$probe" <<<"self-test")"
[[ "$code" == 201 || "$code" == 204 ]] || die "Uploading a test file failed with $code. See /var/log/apache2/nas-uploader-error.log"
if [[ $JELLYFIN_HERE -eq 1 ]] && id jellyfin >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  if as_user jellyfin test -r "$FOLDER/$probe"; then ok "Jellyfin can read uploaded files"; else warn "The jellyfin user can't read uploaded files; check folder permissions."; fi
fi
curl -sk -o /dev/null -X DELETE -u "$WEB_USER:$WEB_PASS" "$TEST_URL$probe" || true
ok "Upload test passed"

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- 7b. Project Assistant helpers
# OCR for scanned PDFs and photos (so the extension can search them), done here instead of on the work computer,
# and a copy of the free speech-to-text model for computers that can't reach huggingface.co.
ASSIST_DIR="$FOLDER/.assistant"
mkdir -p "$ASSIST_DIR"
chown "$RUN_USER:$RUN_GROUP" "$ASSIST_DIR"
OCR_OK=0
if [[ $NO_OCR -eq 0 ]]; then
  say "Setting up OCR for scanned PDFs (Project Assistant)"
  ocr_missing=()
  for p in tesseract-ocr poppler-utils python3; do dpkg -s "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 || ocr_missing+=("$p"); done
  if [[ ${#ocr_missing[@]} -gt 0 ]] && ! { apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq "${ocr_missing[@]}" >/dev/null; }; then
    warn "Couldn't install ${ocr_missing[*]}; OCR skipped (run the script again later)."
  else
    mkdir -p /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader
    here="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" 2>/dev/null && pwd || true)"
    if [[ -n "$here" && -f "$here/assistant-ocr-index.py" ]]; then
      cp "$here/assistant-ocr-index.py" /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py
    else
      curl -fsSL "$RAW_BASE/assistant-ocr-index.py" -o /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py || true
    fi
    # Outlook archives: readpst unpacks .pst files, msgconvert saved .msg emails (optional — OCR works without them).
    mail_missing=()
    for p in pst-utils libemail-outlook-message-perl; do dpkg -s "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 || mail_missing+=("$p"); done
    if [[ ${#mail_missing[@]} -gt 0 ]] && ! apt-get install -y -qq "${mail_missing[@]}" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      warn "Couldn't install ${mail_missing[*]}; .pst/.msg emails won't be unpacked."
    fi
    if python3 -I -c "import ast,sys; ast.parse(open(sys.argv[1]).read())" /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py 2>/dev/null; then
      chmod 755 /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py
      if have_systemd; then
        cat > /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-ocr.service <<UNIT
[Unit]
Description=Project Assistant: OCR scanned PDFs and photos in the project folders
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
User=$RUN_USER
Group=$RUN_GROUP
Nice=19
IOSchedulingClass=idle
ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 -I /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py "$FOLDER" --max-minutes 240
UNIT
        cat > /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-ocr.timer <<UNIT
[Unit]
Description=Project Assistant OCR, every 2 hours

[Timer]
OnBootSec=20min
OnUnitActiveSec=2h
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
UNIT
        systemctl daemon-reload
        systemctl enable --now nas-uploader-ocr.timer >/dev/null 2>&1 && OCR_OK=1
      else
        echo "17 */2 * * * $RUN_USER nice -n 19 /usr/bin/python3 -I /usr/local/lib/nas-uploader/assistant-ocr-index.py '$FOLDER' --max-minutes 100 >/dev/null 2>&1" > /etc/cron.d/nas-uploader-ocr
        OCR_OK=1
      fi
      if [[ $OCR_OK -eq 1 ]]; then ok "OCR runs every 2 hours on the project folders the extension lists (never on your media)"; else warn "Couldn't start the OCR timer."; fi
    else
      warn "Couldn't get assistant-ocr-index.py (put it next to this script); OCR skipped."
    fi
  fi
fi
if [[ $NO_WHISPER -eq 0 ]]; then
  say "Copying the free speech-to-text model to the NAS (about 80 MB, once)"
  MODEL=Xenova/whisper-base.en
  MDIR="$ASSIST_DIR/models/$MODEL"
  mkdir -p "$MDIR/onnx"
  got=1
  for f in config.json generation_config.json preprocessor_config.json tokenizer.json tokenizer_config.json onnx/encoder_model_quantized.onnx onnx/decoder_model_merged_quantized.onnx; do
    [[ -s "$MDIR/$f" ]] && continue
    curl -fsSL --retry 2 "$HF_URL/$MODEL/resolve/main/$f" -o "$MDIR/$f.part" && mv "$MDIR/$f.part" "$MDIR/$f" || { got=0; rm -f "$MDIR/$f.part"; break; }
  done
  chown -R "$RUN_USER:$RUN_GROUP" "$ASSIST_DIR/models"
  if [[ $got -eq 1 ]]; then ok "Speech model on the NAS: the extension uses it when a computer can't reach huggingface.co"; else warn "Couldn't download the speech model (no internet here?). The extension downloads it itself instead."; fi
fi

    else

    fi

    echo "Then check from your phone with Wi-Fi off: $PUBLIC_URL should ask for a login." ;;
  local)
    echo

    echo "--access port-forward when you want to reach it from outside."
    echo "The certificate is self-signed: on each computer, click \"Open the NAS address in a tab\""
    echo "in the extension once and accept the warning." ;;
  funnel)
    echo
    echo "Reachable from anywhere at $PUBLIC_URL with a real certificate. No router changes needed." ;;
esac
if [[ $JELLYFIN_HERE -eq 1 ]]; then
  echo

  echo "so uploads show up right away; otherwise they appear after the next library scan."
fi


===== FILE: server/assistant-ocr-index.py (388 lines) =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Builds the OCR ("deep") index for Project Assistant on the NAS: scanned PDFs, photos and email archives.

The extension indexes project files on the user's computer, but it can't read scanned PDFs or photos of
documents — they have no text layer. This script runs on the HP (or a Pi), next to the mirrored files, and
OCRs exactly those: PDFs whose pages have little or no text, and images. It writes
<root>/.assistant/deep-index.jsonl.gz, which the extension fetches over WebDAV and applies to the same files.
No AI service and no tokens are involved; it uses Tesseract and Poppler on the server.

It also unpacks Outlook mailboxes the extension can't open itself — .pst archives (with readpst) and saved .msg
emails (with msgconvert) — so project emails that never reach Outlook on the web become searchable too. Each email
becomes one "page" of its file, newest first: sender, date, subject, folder, attachment names and the text.

Only the project folders are read: the extension lists them in <root>/.assistant/project-folders.json
(so movies, posters and other files on the NAS are never touched). --folder adds folders by hand.

Folders that exist only on the NAS (for example standards copied from Google Drive) are listed there as
"nasOnly": for those, every document's text is extracted here (PDF, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, text; OCR where a
page has no text), because the work computer has no copy to read.

Each line: {"path": "Projects/x/y.pdf", "size": 123, "mtime": 1700000000000, "method": "ocr",
            "pages": [[1, "text of page 1"], [2, "…"]]}
Files whose size and modification time haven't changed are taken from the previous index (no repeat OCR).

Usage: assistant-ocr-index.py ROOT [--max-pages 300] [--max-minutes 0] [--owner www-data] [--lang eng]
"""
import argparse
import email
import email.policy
import email.utils
import gzip
import html
import re
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time
import zipfile

PDF = {'.pdf'}
IMAGES = {'.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.tif', '.tiff', '.bmp', '.gif', '.webp'}
MAIL = {'.pst', '.msg'}
OFFICE = {'.docx', '.docm', '.xlsx', '.xlsm', '.pptx'}
TEXT = {'.txt', '.md', '.csv', '.tsv'}
MAIL_CHARS_PER_EMAIL = 6000
SKIP_DIRS = {'.assistant', '.mailmirror', '@eadir', '#recycle', '.recycle', 'lost+found', '.snapshot', '.snapshots', 'metadata', '.trickplay'}
MIN_CHARS_PER_PAGE = 60  # below this a PDF page counts as scanned
OUT_REL = '.assistant/deep-index.jsonl.gz'


def log(*a):
    print(time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'), *a, flush=True)


def run(cmd, timeout=300):
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, timeout=timeout)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{cmd[0]} failed: {r.stderr.decode('utf-8', 'replace')[:200]}")
    return r.stdout.decode('utf-8', 'replace')


def pdf_pages_text(path, max_pages):
    """Text layer per page (pdftotext puts a form feed between pages)."""
    try:
        out = run(['pdftotext', '-q', '-l', str(max_pages), '-enc', 'UTF-8', path, '-'], timeout=600)
    except Exception:
        return []
    pages = out.split('\f')
    if pages and not pages[-1].strip():
        pages.pop()
    return pages


def ocr_image(path, lang):
    return run(['tesseract', path, '-', '-l', lang, '--psm', '3'], timeout=300).strip()


def ocr_pdf(path, pages_text, max_pages, lang, deadline):
    """OCR only the pages that have (almost) no text; keep the text layer of the others."""
    out = []
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='ocr-')
    try:
        for i, t in enumerate(pages_text[:max_pages], start=1):
            if deadline and time.time() > deadline:
                break
            if len(t.strip()) >= MIN_CHARS_PER_PAGE:
                out.append([i, t.strip()])
                continue
            png = os.path.join(tmp, f'p{i}')
            run(['pdftoppm', '-r', '200', '-gray', '-png', '-f', str(i), '-l', str(i), '-singlefile', path, png], timeout=300)
            text = ocr_image(png + '.png', lang)
            os.unlink(png + '.png')
            if text:
                out.append([i, text])
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)
    return out


DATE_RE = re.compile(r'[A-Z][a-z]{2}, \d{1,2} [A-Z][a-z]{2} \d{4} \d{1,2}:\d{2}(:\d{2})? [+-]\d{4}')


def mail_date(value):
    for v in (value or '', *(m.group(0) for m in DATE_RE.finditer(value or ''))):
        try:
            d = email.utils.parsedate_to_datetime(v)
            if d:
                return d.timestamp()
        except Exception:
            continue
    return 0


def html_to_text(h):
    h = re.sub(r'(?is)<(script|style).*?</\1>', ' ', h)
    h = re.sub(r'(?i)<br\s*/?>|</p>|</div>|</tr>|</li>', '\n', h)
    return html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', h))


def eml_text(path, folder=''):
    """(timestamp, text) for one .eml file."""
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        msg = email.message_from_binary_file(f, policy=email.policy.default)
    body = ''
    try:
        part = msg.get_body(preferencelist=('plain', 'html'))
        if part is not None:
            body = part.get_content()
            if part.get_content_type() == 'text/html':
                body = html_to_text(body)
    except Exception:
        body = ''
    body = re.sub(r'[ \t]+', ' ', re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', body or '')).strip()
    try:
        attachments = [a.get_filename() for a in msg.iter_attachments() if a.get_filename()]
    except Exception:
        attachments = []
    head = [f"Subject: {msg.get('subject', '') or '(no subject)'}", f"From: {msg.get('from', '')}"]
    if msg.get('to'):
        head.append(f"To: {str(msg.get('to'))[:300]}")
    if msg.get('cc'):
        head.append(f"Cc: {str(msg.get('cc'))[:300]}")
    head.append(f"Date: {msg.get('date', '')}")
    if folder:
        head.append(f'Folder: {folder}')
    if attachments:
        head.append('Attachments: ' + ', '.join(attachments[:20]))
    text = '\n'.join(head) + '\n\n' + body
    return mail_date(str(msg.get('date', ''))), text[:MAIL_CHARS_PER_EMAIL]


def mail_pages(path, ext, max_chars, deadline):
    """Unpacks a .pst or .msg into [[n, email text], ...], newest email first."""
    tmp = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='mail-')
    try:
        if ext == '.pst':
            run(['readpst', '-e', '-q', '-8', '-t', 'e', '-j', '0', '-o', tmp, path], timeout=7200)
        else:
            run(['msgconvert', '--outfile', os.path.join(tmp, 'message.eml'), path], timeout=300)
        mails = []
        for dirpath, _, files in os.walk(tmp):
            for name in files:
                if not name.lower().endswith('.eml'):
                    continue
                if deadline and time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                folder = os.path.relpath(dirpath, tmp).replace(os.sep, '/')
                try:
                    mails.append(eml_text(os.path.join(dirpath, name), '' if folder == '.' else folder))
                except Exception:
                    continue
        mails.sort(key=lambda m: -m[0])
        pages, used = [], 0
        for i, (_, text) in enumerate(mails, start=1):
            if used + len(text) > max_chars:
                break
            pages.append([i, text])
            used += len(text)
        return pages, len(mails)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(tmp, ignore_errors=True)


def xml_text(xml):
    xml = re.sub(r'</(w:p|a:p|row)>', '\n', xml)
    xml = re.sub(r'<(w:tab|w:br)[^>]*/>', ' ', xml)
    return html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', xml))


def office_pages(path, ext):
    """Text of a Word / Excel / PowerPoint file: [[page, text]] (slides and sheets count as pages)."""
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
        names = z.namelist()
        read = lambda n: z.read(n).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
        if ext in ('.docx', '.docm'):
            return [[0, xml_text(read('word/document.xml'))]] if 'word/document.xml' in names else []
        if ext == '.pptx':
            slides = sorted((n for n in names if re.match(r'ppt/slides/slide\d+\.xml$', n)), key=lambda n: int(re.findall(r'\d+', n)[-1]))
            return [[i, xml_text(read(n))] for i, n in enumerate(slides, start=1)]
        shared = []
        if 'xl/sharedStrings.xml' in names:
            shared = [xml_text(si) for si in re.findall(r'<si>(.*?)</si>', read('xl/sharedStrings.xml'), re.S)]
        pages = []
        sheets = sorted((n for n in names if re.match(r'xl/worksheets/sheet\d+\.xml$', n)), key=lambda n: int(re.findall(r'\d+', n)[-1]))
        for i, n in enumerate(sheets, start=1):
            rows = []
            for row in re.findall(r'<row[^>]*>(.*?)</row>', read(n), re.S):
                cells = []
                for attrs, v in re.findall(r'<c([^>]*)>(?:.*?<v>(.*?)</v>)?', row, re.S):
                    if not v:
                        continue
                    cells.append(shared[int(v)] if 't="s"' in attrs and v.isdigit() and int(v) < len(shared) else html.unescape(v))
                if cells:
                    rows.append('\t'.join(cells))
            if rows:
                pages.append([i, '\n'.join(rows)])
        return pages


def full_text_pages(path, ext, max_pages, lang, deadline):
    """All the text of a document on a NAS-only folder (there is no local copy for the extension to read)."""
    if ext in PDF:
        pages_text = pdf_pages_text(path, max_pages)
        if not pages_text:
            return [], 'none'
        if all(len(t.strip()) >= MIN_CHARS_PER_PAGE for t in pages_text):
            return [[i, t.strip()] for i, t in enumerate(pages_text, start=1) if t.strip()], 'text'
        return ocr_pdf(path, pages_text, max_pages, lang, deadline), 'ocr'
    if ext in OFFICE:
        return [p for p in office_pages(path, ext) if p[1].strip()], 'text'
    if ext in TEXT:
        with open(path, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') as f:
            return [[0, f.read(2_000_000)]], 'text'
    if ext in IMAGES:
        t = ocr_image(path, lang)
        return ([[1, t]] if t else []), 'ocr'
    return [], 'none'


def load_previous(out_path):
    prev = {}
    try:
        with gzip.open(out_path, 'rt', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            for line in f:
                try:
                    r = json.loads(line)
                    prev[r['path']] = r
                except Exception:
                    continue
    except FileNotFoundError:
        pass
    except Exception as e:
        log('previous index unreadable, rebuilding:', e)
    return prev


def walk(root, nas_only=False, skip=frozenset()):
    kinds = PDF | IMAGES | MAIL | (OFFICE | TEXT if nas_only else set())
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
        # skip: mirrored folders inside a NAS-only folder (their files come from the computer, read there)
        dirnames[:] = sorted(d for d in dirnames if d.lower() not in SKIP_DIRS and not d.startswith('.') and os.path.join(dirpath, d) not in skip)
        for name in sorted(filenames):
            if name.startswith('.') or name.startswith('~$'):
                continue
            ext = os.path.splitext(name)[1].lower()
            if ext in kinds:
                yield os.path.join(dirpath, name), ext, nas_only


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.split('\n')[0])
    ap.add_argument('root')
    ap.add_argument('--max-pages', type=int, default=300)
    ap.add_argument('--max-minutes', type=float, default=0, help='stop after this long (0 = no limit); the rest is done next run')
    ap.add_argument('--owner', default='', help='chown the index to this user (the WebDAV server user)')
    ap.add_argument('--lang', default='eng', help='Tesseract language(s), e.g. eng+fra')
    ap.add_argument('--folder', action='append', default=[], help='a project folder (NAS path) to include; repeatable')
    ap.add_argument('--max-mail-chars', type=int, default=8_000_000, help='most text kept per mailbox (newest emails first)')
    a = ap.parse_args()
    root = os.path.abspath(a.root)
    out_path = os.path.join(root, OUT_REL)
    folders = list(a.folder)
    nas_only = []
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(root, '.assistant', 'project-folders.json'), encoding='utf-8') as f:
            listed = json.load(f)
            folders += [x for x in listed.get('folders', []) if isinstance(x, str)]
            nas_only += [x for x in listed.get('nasOnly', []) if isinstance(x, str)]
    except FileNotFoundError:
        pass
    except Exception as e:
        log('project-folders.json unreadable:', e)

    def inside_root(x):
        d = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(root, x.strip('/')))
        return d if (d == root or d.startswith(root + os.sep)) and os.path.isdir(d) and d != root else None

    mirrored = list(dict.fromkeys(filter(None, map(inside_root, folders))))
    within = lambda d, parents: any(d == p or d.startswith(p + os.sep) for p in parents)
    scan_dirs = [(d, False) for d in mirrored]
    # A NAS-only folder inside a mirrored one isn't NAS-only (a sync would delete what isn't on the computer).
    scan_dirs += [(d, True) for d in dict.fromkeys(filter(None, map(inside_root, nas_only))) if not within(d, mirrored)]
    if not scan_dirs:
        log('no project folders listed yet (the extension writes .assistant/project-folders.json after its first index run)')
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out_path), exist_ok=True)
    for tool in ('pdftotext', 'pdftoppm', 'tesseract'):
        if not shutil.which(tool):
            log(f'{tool} is not installed (apt-get install poppler-utils tesseract-ocr)')
            return 2
    has_readpst = bool(shutil.which('readpst'))
    has_msgconvert = bool(shutil.which('msgconvert'))
    prev = load_previous(out_path)
    deadline = time.time() + a.max_minutes * 60 if a.max_minutes else 0
    records, reused, ocred, skipped, failed = [], 0, 0, 0, 0
    mirrored_set = frozenset(mirrored)
    for path, ext, full in (item for d, only in scan_dirs for item in walk(d, only, mirrored_set if only else frozenset())):
        rel = os.path.relpath(path, root).replace(os.sep, '/')
        try:
            st = os.stat(path)
        except OSError:
            continue
        mtime = int(st.st_mtime * 1000)
        old = prev.get(rel)
        if old and old.get('size') == st.st_size and old.get('mtime') == mtime:
            records.append(old)
            reused += 1
            continue
        if deadline and time.time() > deadline:
            if old:
                records.append(old)  # stale but better than nothing until the next run
            continue
        try:
            method = 'ocr'
            if full and ext not in MAIL:
                pages, method = full_text_pages(path, ext, a.max_pages, a.lang, deadline)
                if not pages:
                    skipped += 1
                    continue
            elif ext in MAIL:
                if (ext == '.pst' and not has_readpst) or (ext == '.msg' and not has_msgconvert):
                    skipped += 1
                    continue
                pages, total = mail_pages(path, ext, a.max_mail_chars, deadline)
                method = ext[1:]
                if pages:
                    log('mail', rel, f'{len(pages)} of {total} email(s)')
            elif ext in IMAGES:
                text = ocr_image(path, a.lang)
                pages = [[1, text]] if text else []
            else:
                pages_text = pdf_pages_text(path, a.max_pages)
                if not pages_text:
                    skipped += 1
                    continue
                if all(len(t.strip()) >= MIN_CHARS_PER_PAGE for t in pages_text):
                    skipped += 1  # has a proper text layer; the extension reads it itself
                    continue
                pages = ocr_pdf(path, pages_text, a.max_pages, a.lang, deadline)
            if pages:
                records.append({'path': rel, 'size': st.st_size, 'mtime': mtime, 'method': method, 'pages': pages, **({'nasOnly': True} if full else {})})
                ocred += 1
                if method == 'ocr':
                    log('OCR', rel, f'{len(pages)} page(s)')
        except Exception as e:
            failed += 1
            log('failed', rel, e)
    if not scan_dirs and not prev:
        return 0
    tmp = out_path + '.tmp'
    with gzip.open(tmp, 'wt', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        for r in records:
            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')
    os.replace(tmp, out_path)
    if a.owner:
        try:
            shutil.chown(out_path, a.owner, a.owner)
            shutil.chown(os.path.dirname(out_path), a.owner, a.owner)
        except Exception as e:
            log('chown failed:', e)
    log(f'done: {ocred} OCRed or unpacked, {reused} unchanged, {skipped} with a text layer (read by the extension), {failed} failed → {out_path}')
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())


===== FILE: server/setup-drive-copy.sh (184 lines) =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# =============================================================================
#  Copies Google Drive folders (e.g. your S6 / TAC standards) to the NAS every day, so Project Assistant can
#  search them. Runs on the HP's container (where setup-webdav.sh was run). Nothing to install on the work PC.
#
#  It uses a Google "service account": a robot Google account you create once in a web browser, and share the
#  Drive folders with. No password or browser sign-in is needed on the HP.
#
#  One-time, in any browser (about 5 minutes):
#    1. https://console.cloud.google.com → create a project (any name) → "APIs & Services" → Library →
#       enable "Google Drive API".
#    2. "IAM & Admin" → Service accounts → Create → (no roles needed) → Done. Open it → Keys → Add key →
#       JSON → a .json file downloads. Copy its text.
#    3. In Google Drive, share each folder you want (e.g. "Standards") with the service account's e-mail
#       (…@….iam.gserviceaccount.com) as Viewer. Copy each folder's ID from its address bar
#       (https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/<THIS-PART>).
#
#  Then on the HP container:
#    nano /root/drive-key.json                      # paste the key, save
#    bash setup-drive-copy.sh --key /root/drive-key.json --folder <FOLDER-ID-or-URL> --to /mnt/mirror/Standards
#    (repeat --folder/--to pairs for more folders)
#
#  Options:
#    --key FILE        the service account key (.json); it is moved to /etc/nas-uploader-drive/drive-key.json
#    --folder ID|URL   a shared Drive folder (repeatable, each followed by --to)
#    --to DIR          where its copy goes on the NAS (inside the WebDAV folder)
#    --sync            also delete files from the copy that were deleted in Drive (default: only add/update)
#    --now             copy once right away (also done at the end of setup)
#    --uninstall       stop the daily copy (copied files are kept)
#
#  Then in the extension: Settings → Project folders → "Folders that are only on the NAS": add the NAS path
#  (e.g. /Standards). The HP reads the documents (with OCR) and they become searchable in meetings and drafts.
# =============================================================================
set -euo pipefail

KEY=""
SYNC=0
NOW=0
UNINSTALL=0
FOLDERS=()
DESTS=()
STATE_DIR=/etc/nas-uploader
KEY_DIR=/etc/nas-uploader-drive # readable by the WebDAV user's group only (rclone runs as that user)
JOBS="$KEY_DIR/drive-copy.list"
RUNNER=/usr/local/sbin/nas-uploader-drive-copy
# For automated tests only: copy from a local folder instead of Google Drive.
TEST_SOURCE="${NAS_UPLOADER_DRIVE_TEST_SOURCE:-}"

say() { printf '\033[1;36m==>\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
ok() { printf '\033[1;32m ✓\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
warn() { printf '\033[1;33m !\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
die() { printf '\033[1;31m ✗ %s\033[0m\n' "$*" >&2; exit 1; }

while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    --key) KEY="${2:?}"; shift 2 ;;
    --folder) FOLDERS+=("${2:?}"); shift 2 ;;
    --to) DESTS+=("${2:?}"); shift 2 ;;
    --sync) SYNC=1; shift ;;
    --now) NOW=1; shift ;;
    --uninstall) UNINSTALL=1; shift ;;
    -h|--help) sed -n '2,36p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,2\}//'; exit 0 ;;
    *) die "Unknown option: $1 (see --help)" ;;
  esac
done
[[ $EUID -eq 0 ]] || die "Run this as root (in the Proxmox container console you already are root)."
have_systemd() { [[ -d /run/systemd/system ]]; }

if [[ $UNINSTALL -eq 1 ]]; then
  if have_systemd; then systemctl disable --now nas-uploader-drive.timer >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; fi
  rm -f /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-drive.service /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-drive.timer /etc/cron.d/nas-uploader-drive "$RUNNER" "$JOBS"
  rm -rf /var/lib/nas-uploader-drive
  ok "Daily Drive copy stopped. The copied files are still on the NAS; the key is in $KEY_DIR/drive-key.json (delete it if you like)."
  exit 0
fi

[[ ${#FOLDERS[@]} -gt 0 ]] || die "Give at least one --folder <ID or URL> --to <NAS folder>. See --help."
[[ ${#FOLDERS[@]} -eq ${#DESTS[@]} ]] || die "Each --folder needs its own --to."
RUN_USER="$(cat "$STATE_DIR/run-user" 2>/dev/null || echo www-data)"
RUN_GROUP="$(id -gn "$RUN_USER" 2>/dev/null || echo www-data)"
NAS_ROOT="$(cat "$STATE_DIR/folder" 2>/dev/null || true)"
mkdir -p "$KEY_DIR"
chown "root:$RUN_GROUP" "$KEY_DIR" && chmod 750 "$KEY_DIR"
if [[ -z "$TEST_SOURCE" ]]; then
  if [[ -n "$KEY" ]]; then
    [[ -f "$KEY" ]] || die "Key file not found: $KEY"
    python3 -I -c 'import json,sys; d=json.load(open(sys.argv[1])); assert d.get("type")=="service_account" and d.get("client_email")' "$KEY" 2>/dev/null \
      || die "$KEY isn't a service account key (.json from Google Cloud → Service accounts → Keys)."
    install -m 640 -o root -g "$RUN_GROUP" "$KEY" "$KEY_DIR/drive-key.json"
    [[ "$(realpath "$KEY")" == "$KEY_DIR/drive-key.json" ]] || rm -f "$KEY"
  fi
  [[ -f "$KEY_DIR/drive-key.json" ]] || die "Give the service account key with --key FILE."
  SA_EMAIL="$(python3 -I -c 'import json,sys; print(json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["client_email"])' "$KEY_DIR/drive-key.json")"
fi

say "Installing rclone"
command -v rclone >/dev/null || { apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y -qq rclone >/dev/null; } || die "Couldn't install rclone."
ok "rclone $(rclone version 2>/dev/null | head -1 | awk '{print $2}')"

# Copied files belong to the user the WebDAV server runs as, so they look like any upload.
: > "$JOBS.new"
for i in "${!FOLDERS[@]}"; do
  id="${FOLDERS[$i]}"
  id="${id##*/folders/}"; id="${id%%\?*}"; id="${id%%/*}"
  [[ "$id" =~ ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}$ ]] || [[ -n "$TEST_SOURCE" ]] || die "\"${FOLDERS[$i]}\" doesn't look like a Drive folder ID or link."
  dest="$(realpath -m "${DESTS[$i]}")"
  if [[ -n "$NAS_ROOT" && "$dest" != "$NAS_ROOT"/* ]]; then die "$dest is not inside the folder the NAS shares ($NAS_ROOT), so the extension couldn't see it."; fi
  mkdir -p "$dest"
  chown "$RUN_USER:" "$dest" 2>/dev/null || true
  printf '%s\t%s\n' "$id" "$dest" >> "$JOBS.new"
done
mv "$JOBS.new" "$JOBS"
chmod 600 "$JOBS"

MODE=copy
[[ $SYNC -eq 1 ]] && MODE=sync
WORK_DIR=/var/lib/nas-uploader-drive
mkdir -p "$WORK_DIR" && touch "$WORK_DIR/rclone.conf"
chown -R "$RUN_USER:$RUN_GROUP" "$WORK_DIR" && chmod 700 "$WORK_DIR"
cat > "$RUNNER" <<RUN
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Copies each Drive folder in $JOBS to the NAS (written by setup-drive-copy.sh).
set -uo pipefail
status=0
while IFS=\$'\\t' read -r id dest; do
  [[ -n "\$id" ]] || continue
  if [[ -n "$TEST_SOURCE" ]]; then src="$TEST_SOURCE/\$id"; else src=":drive,scope=drive.readonly,service_account_file=$KEY_DIR/drive-key.json,root_folder_id=\$id:"; fi
  # Google Docs/Sheets/Slides are saved as Word/Excel/PowerPoint so they can be read and searched.
  # rclone runs as the WebDAV user, with its own home (it must not look at root's).
  setpriv --reuid="$RUN_USER" --regid="\$(id -g "$RUN_USER")" --init-groups -- \\
    env HOME="$WORK_DIR" RCLONE_CONFIG="$WORK_DIR/rclone.conf" \\
    rclone $MODE "\$src" "\$dest" --drive-export-formats docx,xlsx,pptx,pdf --transfers 4 --log-level NOTICE || status=1
done < "$JOBS"
exit \$status
RUN
chmod 700 "$RUNNER"

if have_systemd; then
  cat > /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-drive.service <<UNIT
[Unit]
Description=Project Assistant: copy Google Drive folders to the NAS
After=network-online.target
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=oneshot
Nice=10
ExecStart=$RUNNER
UNIT
  cat > /etc/systemd/system/nas-uploader-drive.timer <<UNIT
[Unit]
Description=Copy Google Drive folders to the NAS, daily

[Timer]
OnCalendar=*-*-* 03:17
OnBootSec=30min
Persistent=true

[Install]
WantedBy=timers.target
UNIT
  systemctl daemon-reload
  systemctl enable --now nas-uploader-drive.timer >/dev/null
else
  echo "17 3 * * * root $RUNNER >/dev/null 2>&1" > /etc/cron.d/nas-uploader-drive
fi
ok "Daily copy set up ($MODE): $(wc -l < "$JOBS") folder(s)"

say "Copying now"
if "$RUNNER"; then
  ok "Copied."
else
  if [[ -z "$TEST_SOURCE" ]]; then
    warn "The copy reported errors. Check that each folder is shared with $SA_EMAIL (Viewer) and that the Google Drive API is enabled for its project."
  else
    warn "The copy reported errors."
  fi
  exit 1
fi
echo
echo "In the extension: Settings → Project folders → \"Folders that are only on the NAS\", add:"
while IFS=$'\t' read -r _ dest; do
  if [[ -n "$NAS_ROOT" ]]; then echo "  ${dest#"$NAS_ROOT"}"; else echo "  $dest (as a path inside the folder the NAS shares)"; fi
done < "$JOBS"
